## 2026.10.5


### v2026.10.5

For formatted release notes, [read this release on the docs site](https://docs.openclaw.ai/releases/2026.10.5).

Draft checkpoint: `release/2026.10.5` at `6b4b2a945d5ff157920efa663d1c6192a9a1e717`, observed October 15, 2026, at 19:07:09 UTC. Final-tag coverage is pending.

Skill Workshop can now save procedures your agent learns as reusable skills, with saved versions and Undo so you can review or reverse changes. Muse Wonder Standard also joins the model choices.

For very long conversations, OpenClaw now limits how much history it summarizes at once. Eligible direct OpenAI and Anthropic API-key connections can also use the provider’s own compaction to shorten what the model carries forward. An agent can hear back about delegated work interrupted by a restart and inspect its retained history before continuing. The Mac app’s Retry action can also finish recovery for the selected Gateway installation and its plugins.

**Release scale:** 2,144 pull requests, 378 direct commits, and 590 contributors.

Jump to [Skills](https://docs.openclaw.ai/releases/2026.10.5#skills), [Memory](https://docs.openclaw.ai/releases/2026.10.5#memory), [Models and Providers](https://docs.openclaw.ai/releases/2026.10.5#models-and-providers), [Updates and Maintenance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/releases/2026.10.5#updates-and-maintenance), [Native Apps](https://docs.openclaw.ai/releases/2026.10.5#native-apps), [Messaging](https://docs.openclaw.ai/releases/2026.10.5#messaging), [Web UI](https://docs.openclaw.ai/releases/2026.10.5#web-ui), [Installation and Onboarding](https://docs.openclaw.ai/releases/2026.10.5#installation-and-onboarding), [Automations and Scheduling](https://docs.openclaw.ai/releases/2026.10.5#automations-and-scheduling), [Browser and Computer Use](https://docs.openclaw.ai/releases/2026.10.5#browser-and-computer-use), [Plugins and Integrations](https://docs.openclaw.ai/releases/2026.10.5#plugins-and-integrations), [Security and Privacy](https://docs.openclaw.ai/releases/2026.10.5#security-and-privacy), [Quality-of-Life Improvements](https://docs.openclaw.ai/releases/2026.10.5#quality-of-life-improvements), or [Other Bug Fixes](https://docs.openclaw.ai/releases/2026.10.5#other-bug-fixes).

#### Installation and Onboarding

Setup gives clearer choices and recovery advice, while package installation fixes address disk use and commands left pointing at removed versions.




##### Setup and provider connections


Hosted-only Ollama setup now uses the dedicated [Ollama Cloud](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/ollama-cloud) choice. For scripted setup, use `openclaw onboard --auth-choice ollama-cloud` with cloud credentials. The old Ollama setup command with an `ollama.com` base URL is rejected before discovery or a requested reset changes your settings. [Cloud + Local](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/ollama) remains available through a local Ollama server.

Agents using a different model can consult the built-in setup and repair helper, including after a benign plugin reload. When a helper request fails, its recovery advice distinguishes a missing setup from a timeout or unavailable runtime, so a temporary failure no longer sends you straight back through onboarding.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Setup guidance**

- Hosted-only Ollama onboarding uses the dedicated cloud provider and rejects the obsolete local-provider setup route before reset. Existing saved configurations are not migrated. [#167433](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167433). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Volcengine and BytePlus examples in the [custom-provider guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/model-providers/custom-providers) now match the existing model catalogs. [#161909](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161909). Thanks [Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- [Multiple-Gateway guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/multiple-gateways) includes the sandbox listener when planning separate ports. [#164275](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164275). Thanks [Satoshi-Kusumoto](https://github.com/Satoshi-Kusumoto) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

**Bug fixes**

- OpenClaw-managed recovery and Node-driven npm/pnpm installs keep install scripts on the selected compatible [Node runtime](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/node). [#164677](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164677). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [jarvisnextgeneration20-lang](https://github.com/jarvisnextgeneration20-lang).
- Installing shell completion preserves significant trailing spaces in existing shell-profile commands. Commands changed by an earlier installation still need manual restoration. [#164785](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164785). Thanks [yetval](https://github.com/yetval) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Setup and repair helper calls work with a non-default caller model and can be repeated after a benign plugin reload. [#166235](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166235). Thanks [PollyBot13](https://github.com/PollyBot13), [Colton-Harris](https://github.com/Colton-Harris), [tyler194653](https://github.com/tyler194653) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Helper errors give recovery advice for the actual failure, reserving onboarding instructions for missing verified setup. [#166234](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166234). Thanks [PollyBot13](https://github.com/PollyBot13) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), with reporting and investigation from [Colton-Harris](https://github.com/Colton-Harris), [tyler194653](https://github.com/tyler194653), [stemkat100](https://github.com/stemkat100), [MaikiMolto](https://github.com/MaikiMolto), [explainanalyze](https://github.com/explainanalyze), [edith-vitaetpax](https://github.com/edith-vitaetpax), [spiral-cmd](https://github.com/spiral-cmd), [FtlC-ian](https://github.com/FtlC-ian), [jj5638521](https://github.com/jj5638521), [iamade](https://github.com/iamade), [dh-js](https://github.com/dh-js), [JoPaMu](https://github.com/JoPaMu), [skorfmann](https://github.com/skorfmann) and [ryanography](https://github.com/ryanography).






##### Package installs and service launchers


Global npm installs avoid native packages intended for other operating systems and processors, reducing installed disk use on affected npm versions. Existing oversized installations are not automatically pruned and may need a [manual reinstall](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/update-methods#alternative-manual-npm-pnpm-or-bun).

New or repaired pnpm Gateway services follow the active package after replacement. Older services that point at a deleted version still need repair; on [Windows](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/windows), run `openclaw gateway install --force` from the current installation. The pnpm 12 launcher fix must already be in the updater performing the update. If the `openclaw` command is already broken, [back up and reinstall](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) the same installed version using the same account and global directories.

After a Homebrew Node upgrade removes the old executable, command tools and locally launched MCP servers can use an available matching replacement. [Restart the Gateway](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/node) to move it and workers that require its exact Node runtime onto the new version.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Package installs and updates**

- Global npm packages retain native dependencies for the host platform instead of unrelated systems. [#165997](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165997). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [tommyfive](https://github.com/tommyfive) and [kenmege](https://github.com/kenmege).
- Managed npm installs honor retry and timeout settings with uppercase or mixed-case environment-variable names, including zero retries. [#165409](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165409). Thanks [Chessing234](https://github.com/Chessing234).
- npm-global updates driven by an updater containing this fix avoid scanning and retaining unrelated neighboring tools while preserving required dependencies. [#169551](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169551). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant) and [holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen).
- pnpm 12 updates keep command launchers pointed at the activated installation after temporary files are removed, including with symlinked global bin folders. Failed launcher checks warn with reinstall guidance. [#170131](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170131). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant) and [k-sekula](https://github.com/k-sekula).

**Service launchers and Node**

- Newly written Gateway launchers for verified pnpm global installs follow package replacement, with forced reinstallation guidance for stale Windows tasks. [#167905](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167905). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [SMF12138](https://github.com/SMF12138).
- New or repaired pnpm service definitions retain stable launch paths, and updates recognize installations running from nested pnpm package directories. See [update methods](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/update-methods). [#170003](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170003). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [xilopaint](https://github.com/xilopaint) and [k-sekula](https://github.com/k-sekula).
- Command helpers and stdio MCP can find a matching stable Homebrew Node path after the old executable is removed. [#166222](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166222). Thanks [jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [mightyx](https://github.com/mightyx).
- Homebrew Node recovery also covers nested command relays, while workers requiring the original runtime give restart guidance. [#166430](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166430). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [mightyx](https://github.com/mightyx).

**Source builds**

- Source updates driven by an updater containing this fix can use locally cached full commits without unnecessary remote discovery and accept SHA-256 commit IDs. Missing clone contents can still require a download. [#165060](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165060). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Full source builds compile overlapping public TypeScript declarations together, reducing repeated compiler work. [#165271](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165271). Thanks [huntharo](https://github.com/huntharo) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Dev-channel updates can prepare a checkout before its dependencies are installed, avoiding an early missing `@openclaw/fs-safe` error. [#165347](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165347). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway status from a source checkout reuses its prepared runtime when input contents still match, even if Git marks the files modified. [#166864](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166864). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).






##### Container installs


Docker source builds include the workspace manifests needed for dependency validation while keeping optional plugin selection intact.

For npm installations baked into a Docker image, an updater containing the fix can make a verified recovery copy when Docker refuses to rename the old package. This step happens after the Gateway stops. Installations still using the 2026.9.7 or 2026.9.8 updater need an image rebuild or the documented [manual first update](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery).



###### Sources and complete change list


**Bug fixes**

- Docker dependency installation supplies all workspace manifests required by pnpm's frozen-lockfile validation while installing only required and selected plugins for production. [b7e788b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b7e788b23e7c41c8d78edc058ff15a885adac635).
- npm package updates can handle Docker image-layer rename failures by verifying a separate rollback copy before removing the original package. [#164769](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164769). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [potterdigital](https://github.com/potterdigital).







#### Web UI

The dashboard makes it easier to follow conversations and delegated work, with more useful chat controls, clearer task status, and improvements to shared conversations, Workboard, and voice.




##### Writing and editing messages


The [chat composer](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) keeps your submitted prompt and familiar layout visible while a new conversation starts. Android browser users can choose more file types and open the camera directly, while file-link tooltips let desktop users copy a full path without opening its preview. The command-approval editor also asks before discarding unsaved changes when you switch targets.

The new-conversation shortcut has changed. **Cmd/Ctrl+Enter opens the new conversation; add Shift to start it in the background.** Confirming text with an input method no longer starts a conversation accidentally.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Message controls and attachments**

- Confirm before discarding unsaved [command-approval settings](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/exec-approvals) when switching targets. [#149181](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149181). Thanks [tommyjoseph](https://github.com/tommyjoseph), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for reporting and investigation, and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for validation.
- Copy a file's full path from its chat tooltip. [#164431](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164431). Thanks [jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen) and [vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Open a typed folder with Enter in New Session and fall back to home when the starting workspace is missing. [#165446](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165446). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep input-method confirmation from submitting New Session or losing its draft. [#165919](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165919). Thanks [ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Confirm saved approval decisions without waiting for notification delivery. [#166396](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166396). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Select STL and other files in Android web chat without renaming them; existing file-size and interpretation limits still apply. [#166402](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166402). Thanks [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
- Clear the submitted collaborator draft preview for shortcut, goal, and command-argument sends in shared chat. [#166824](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166824). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Use Cmd/Ctrl+Enter to open a new conversation and Shift+Cmd/Ctrl+Enter to start it in the background. [#167019](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167019). Thanks [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Show optional question cards more compactly, with the question directly visible. [#168040](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168040). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Open device photo capture directly from Take photo in recognized Android browsers. [#168049](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168049). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

**Chat display and corrections**

- Preserve the header, submitted prompt, mobile navigation, and saved splits while a new conversation starts; sending remains disabled until it is ready. [#166865](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166865), [5a9a6d3](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5a9a6d3ae51a89c5fd923c5aab83e1ba88c13a0a). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- Preserve exact-message and stale-edit checks when saving transcript corrections. [1cd6492](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1cd64928a36671d63aea47d70faf441deef2a2a1).

##### Find conversations and follow their work


The restored [Subagents and Processes panels](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/panels) let you inspect a delegated conversation or recent command output beside the parent chat and stop a specific task. Child names, activity, waiting counts, and failure notices make it easier to see who is still working and open the conversation that needs attention. Child transcripts are view-only, and process inspection needs an available compatible worker.

The [sidebar](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/sessions-and-sidebar) also makes larger collections easier to navigate, with agent search and pinning, clearer controls, and smaller history pages around the message you selected. In Appearance settings, you can choose an agent avatar or an unlocked Lobsterdex character for the browser tab. Lobsterdex collections stay in that browser and do not sync between devices.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Follow delegated work**

- Restore Subagents and Processes panels with child transcripts, bounded recent command output, and task-specific Stop controls. [#164061](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164061). Thanks [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Collapse finished processes by default; click Finished to inspect them again. Reloading collapses the group again. [#164776](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164776). Thanks [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Show when a reply is waiting for delegated work, including remaining children and a link to them, and show unfinished direct-child counts while the parent works when Swarm is enabled. [#165378](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165378), [#165477](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165477). Thanks [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for prior waiting-state work.
- Label child activity with its current state, duration, and conversation link, keeping launch counts distinct. [#165725](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165725). Thanks [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Open the child list from its count, then inspect a child's transcript beside the parent; narrow panes open the full view. [#166142](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166142). Thanks [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Restore unfinished child names, activity, and direct conversation navigation. [#168102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168102). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Show delegated failures above the composer with a name, diagnostic, and Open session action; align populated notices with the composer and remove empty-notice gaps. [#166224](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166224), [#166312](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166312), [#166347](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166347). Thanks [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Wait for the first child-session read before showing an expired-child timeout notice. [#167232](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167232). Thanks [jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Exclude archived children from child badges while keeping those conversations restorable. [#165160](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165160). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [tre1d](https://github.com/tre1d).
- Handle a child being deleted during refresh without a false changing-list error. [#166128](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166128). Thanks [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Clear eligible hidden-child unread dots when their parent or Home is read, preserving failed runs and manual reminders in that flow. [#165792](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165792). Thanks [holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for investigation, verification, and integration.
- Avoid false attention warnings while settings questions or commands are waiting; genuine delivery failures still show attention. [#166124](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166124). Thanks [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

Thanks [rogeriochaves](https://github.com/rogeriochaves), [llmsommelier](https://github.com/llmsommelier), and [fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev) for the earlier report and recovery work.

- Load smaller long-history pages around the selected message, with separate bounded inspection, copy, and download of referenced full outputs. [#166263](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166263). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

[#166784](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166784), [#166981](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166981). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

[#167163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167163), [#167174](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167174). **Find, name, and organize conversations**

- Select all agents or a named agent from either sidebar view, with readable main-session status. [#150587](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150587). Thanks [vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for prior sidebar work.

- Preserve inbox dismissal when a recap, badge, or status refresh arrives at the same time. [#167689](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167689). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Show duplicate-name errors beside the rename field. [#168034](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168034). Thanks [romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda) and [vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).

**Tab icons, links, and display details**

- Choose an agent avatar as a personal browser-tab icon, retaining activity dots. [#165201](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165201). Thanks [vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski) and [jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).

- Translate tab-icon controls and the local-collection notice across existing locales. [#167289](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167289), [#167307](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167307).
- Retain the selected icon in Firefox during appearance or activity updates, and offer square, rounded, or circular agent artwork. Rounded and circular shapes crop the edges. [#167759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167759), [#167809](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167809). Thanks [jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Reuse unchanged avatar thumbnails. [#166429](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166429). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- Order the compact System widget as CPU, memory, then Gateway ping. [#165393](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165393). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

##### Read and reconnect to the dashboard


Chat keeps replies visible as live output becomes saved history, preserves live tool progress through history refreshes, and follows late changes to message size when you are reading the latest output. Completed tool results use shorter previews in history downloads, with Show full output available when you need the rest. Saved dashboards restore their cached title and panel arrangement before showing their widgets.

[Reconnect handling](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/offline-and-reconnect) pauses automatic reads during announced Gateway restarts and avoids disconnecting just because a busy Gateway delays a heartbeat. If an older tab is stuck on Server updated, **save unsent work and reload the browser once** to acquire the repair. [Themes](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/theme) can also choose dashboard names, icons, and working indicators; CRT and Phosphor use quieter terminal styling.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Read chat and inspect results**

- Keep replies visible and the reading position steady while live output is handed over to saved history. [1f9022d](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1f9022d2af0336ede45b249f259e820e25f0d91b).
- Reconcile saved assistant commentary with live updates so each observation appears once and later output stays in order. [#158459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158459). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Preserve live tool progress after a history refresh when the saved call has no result yet; a history-only call remains unknown until more is known. [#165392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165392). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Count completed Code Mode steps whose nested work contains only hidden routine updates, and retain a lone completed step's title with one-click details. [#165358](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165358), [#165426](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165426). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Keep an answer resumed after a subagent wait in one chat block, with whole-request timing when complete history is loaded. Parent token totals exclude child usage. [#165542](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165542). Thanks [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Keep activity and work-summary outcome badges on one line beside long headlines. [#165415](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165415). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoid unnecessary redraws while restoring a saved reading position. [#165101](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165101). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [Yuyorman](https://github.com/Yuyorman).
- Follow the true end after late message-size changes while leaving scrolled-up readers in place. [#166261](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166261). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Download shorter completed-tool previews in chat history; Show full output retrieves the complete result within its existing display limits. Stored transcripts and exports are unchanged. [#167896](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167896). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Start [artifact downloads](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/media-playback) without first validating unrelated messages in a long conversation. [#165030](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165030). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Put Copy and Download beside managed chat images in Image actions, leaving the preview unobscured and available for full-screen viewing. [#166829](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166829). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Finish new WebChat responses with one card per committed attachment, avoiding false media errors and duplicate cards. Previously cached cards are unchanged. [#169093](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169093). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep readable labels and following text in previews around Markdown links with nested brackets or complex destinations. [#166653](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166653). Thanks [jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [rudycelekli](https://github.com/rudycelekli), and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Shorten shared-conversation titles and messages without splitting paired emoji characters. [#141240](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141240). Thanks [hugenshen](https://github.com/hugenshen), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee) for the related boundary finding.
- Show familiar tool labels and authored command titles in Claude and Gemini CLI channel progress. [#167274](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167274). Thanks [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).

**Load and reconnect**

- Reuse verified session updates to reduce repeated list downloads and keep dashboard pages loaded. [#164764](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164764). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoid repeating model and account catalogs in chat metadata downloads. [#165190](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165190). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Load Devices and Talk settings text when those views need it, slightly reducing the initial download. [#164735](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164735), [#165228](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165228). Thanks [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Let chat history and session lists load while unrelated request preparation is queued. [#166122](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166122). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Stop search from asking users to wait for indexing that has already completed. [#165879](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165879). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep already-saved chat history and metadata readable while transcript searches wait on a stalled database writer. [#167446](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167446). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuse compatible live plugins during cold tool discovery rather than loading them repeatedly during reconnect. [#167374](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167374). Thanks [jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Pause read acknowledgements during Gateway restarts and pace temporary retries. [#167020](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167020). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Pause coordinated initial-loading reads during restart or suspension, then resume within their existing deadlines. Writes are not replayed. [#167407](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167407). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduce idle name and avatar requests and pause sidebar, bootstrap, and Automations reads during announced restarts. Direct identity-file edits without a refresh event need a reload or reconnect. [#167968](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167968). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuse retained versioned fonts, themes, and manifests in returning tabs, and privately cache uploaded avatars at their exact revision. [#167993](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167993). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restore a saved dashboard's cached title and panel arrangement before display, with one loading surface until its widgets are ready. [#168073](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168073). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Repair Refresh for chats stuck on Server updated, preserving their destination; tabs still running old code need one ordinary browser reload first. Unsent-work and reload safeguards still apply. [#168941](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168941). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserve the same [chat link](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/urls), draft, and dashboard options during automatic recovery reloads instead of opening Not found. [#169194](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169194). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Let accepted session-list updates from queued Swarm launches finish publishing after their producing task ends, and wait for accepted updates before shutdown closes clients. [#170231](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170231), [#170555](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170555). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep the dashboard connected when a busy Gateway delays application heartbeats. Individual request timeouts remain; a connection that passes transport pings but blocks application messages may wait for socket close or tab activation to recover. [#171253](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171253). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Repair static-asset permissions for Control UI containers running under arbitrary users, including OpenShift. [#165880](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165880). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [Conan-Scott](https://github.com/Conan-Scott).

##### Shared conversations and device reconnects


Reopening a private conversation shows that its access check is loading instead of briefly saying the chat is unavailable. Shared-session requests can keep valid access through unrelated display updates, and member lists no longer wait on unrelated display refreshes. Loaded agent names and avatars stay visible while identity reads pause during a restart.

Active chats can continue through unrelated device pairing and ordinary reconnects from the same device. Explicit revocation, unpairing, and permission changes still stop affected work. Deleting an active conversation also sends connected clients the normal cancellation event with deletion as the reason.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Shared access and deletion**

- Keep current access checks during manual history compaction and membership or artifact reads, and report unreadable device identities in the integrated session changes. [2770dae](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2770dae0e6cf70bfcf617507ea20eba0ce8ef18e).
- Use recent membership updates in shared-session lookups instead of rejecting changes already included in the read. [d511ab9](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d511ab92b63ae9709ff13eb87da9c4707060dbc4).
- Restore authorized message and attachment submission in affected existing global sessions by correcting the session identity check. [e6b93ae](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/e6b93ae77b717e37c879833057ce38b52734d253).
- Preserve still-valid sharing access when a waiting request resumes after a display update or a later transcript message; changed sharing facts still invalidate access. [f6e6805](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f6e6805b99b7a99b657d63ccd4e1f11ab96a8f0d).
- Load sharing members without waiting for unrelated session display or worker-placement refreshes. [#165344](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165344). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Record reply cancellation before session deletion retires the reply, giving connected clients deletion as its stop reason. [9564061](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9564061ab79c433d2d0c9f3fc902e3ccd579a8cb).

**Session lists and reconnects**

- Share repeated work between equivalent permitted session-list views and update affected device-status rows without reloading the whole roster. [#165150](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165150). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuse unchanged list rows during unrelated updates, and reuse prepared session facts during large searches and row refreshes. [#165464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165464), [#165834](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165834). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuse unchanged session-list rows while unrelated subagents are active, continuing to refresh current status and permissions. [#165932](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165932). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserve loaded names and avatars and pace failed identity refreshes during Gateway restarts. [#165443](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165443). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Show access-check loading when reopening a private [conversation link](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/urls), with reload or login guidance if checking fails. [#167097](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167097). Thanks [jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Keep active turns running through unrelated device pairing and ordinary same-device reconnects, while enforcing revocation, unpairing, and changed permissions. [#169457](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169457). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).






##### Workboard and widgets


[Workboard](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/workboard) adds sidebar pinning and confirmed board deletion, easier note selection and editing, and clearer reasons when individual cards cannot be dispatched. Sessions boards hide Home, automation, and system conversations by default, and follow the whole task's outcome while a model falls back. Known pull-request placement stays visible with a stale-data warning during GitHub outages.

The Board agent opens in its board dock without creating another ordinary conversation-list entry. **An existing board creates a fresh dock conversation the next time you open its Board agent.** Its old conversation and history remain available for optional archiving; that history is not transferred to the new conversation.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Organize and edit boards**

- Pin boards and confirm deletion from keyboard-accessible sidebar menus. Default boards and boards with cards remain protected; deleting a Sessions board preserves its conversations. [#165020](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165020). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Hide Home, automation, and system conversations on Sessions boards by default, including existing boards, without deleting them. The Board agent can include Home or automation again. [#165076](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165076). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Open the Board agent in its dock without adding ordinary session entries or inflating people counts. Older boards start a fresh dock conversation while retaining their old history separately. [#165319](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165319). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Select and copy note text without entering edit mode, and use a larger resizable editor for longer notes. [#166625](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166625). Thanks [rogeriochaves](https://github.com/rogeriochaves), [llmsommelier](https://github.com/llmsommelier), and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Display Color instead of a raw translation key in the column color selector. [#167082](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167082). Thanks [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Preserve columns saved elsewhere when changing a Sessions board's appearance, and report invalid status filters clearly. Concurrent intentional column edits still need coordination. [#167348](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167348). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

**Dispatch and task outcomes**

- Refuse to reuse a retained [managed checkout](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) with a different explicit base-reference string, preserving the checkout and explaining how to continue. An unchanged branch name does not refresh it to a newer commit. [#164829](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164829). Thanks [ArcueidMP](https://github.com/ArcueidMP), [mightyx](https://github.com/mightyx), and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Show live activity and readable message previews, link known pull requests, and retain known PR placement with a warning when GitHub cannot refresh it. [#165189](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165189). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- List each failed card's dispatch reason and direct eligible outside-workspace cases to the existing [admin approval request](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/workboard). Restricted cards gain no extra permission. [#167427](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167427). Thanks [Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668), [mightyx](https://github.com/mightyx), and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Follow the whole task's result through model fallback rather than showing false failure or premature review. Session-only links may settle on the next minute sweep. [#168215](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168215). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223), [asikorskiy](https://github.com/asikorskiy), and [ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin).

**Load boards and widgets**

- Avoid downloading unchanged cards and Sessions-board snapshots repeatedly, sharing repeated work for equivalent permitted views. Edits, reconnects, and filter changes still refresh them. [#165058](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165058), [#165141](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165141). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuse an unchanged authorized Sessions-board snapshot before rebuilding its roster, then reuse unchanged rows when individual sessions change. [#165698](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165698), [#165896](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165896). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduce board-snapshot and widget-document read overhead under host load. [#166403](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166403). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Load the full Workboard controller when the board is opened and offer a reload message if loading fails. [#167006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167006). Thanks [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Move database work for plugin administration and first durable Board writes off the Gateway's main thread. [#167702](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167702). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).






##### Links and GitHub previews


GitHub references in chat can use a matching explicit repository link in the same paragraph, so shorthand links and their previews point to the intended repository. The [GitHub reader](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/github) also displays named emoji shortcodes in descriptions and comments. Preview requests share repeated work and stop waiting when their deadline expires.

[Visitor Access](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/reference/visitor-access) uses configured Gateway GitHub credentials for account lookups and gives clearer rate-limit guidance. A rejected configured token stops invitations and revocation by login. Repair or remove that token, or revoke by grant or profile ID; the lookup does not silently retry anonymously.



###### Sources and complete change list


- Share concurrent GitHub preview requests and refresh cached results after one minute. Publication-account discovery can return a retryable timeout after five seconds. [#165094](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165094). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Use configured Gateway GitHub credentials for visitor account lookups, with distinct invalid-login, missing-account, and rate-limit guidance. Rejected configured tokens require repair or removal rather than anonymous retry. [#165368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165368). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Resolve matching shorthand issue or PR references using an explicit repository link in the same paragraph; conflicting references stay plain text. [#165849](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165849). Thanks [jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for built-Gateway verification.
- Include queue time in link-preview deadlines and reuse matching preview-image requests. [#166474](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166474). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Overlap independent GitHub hovercard checks and avatar work while retaining public-repository checks. [#167597](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167597). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Display named emoji shortcodes in GitHub descriptions and comments while leaving code, URLs, escaped shortcodes, and unknown names literal. [#167830](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167830). Thanks [jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).






##### Terminal tabs


Moving [terminal tabs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/panels) between bottom and right docks keeps their selection and retained output. Normal shell exits close their tab and an otherwise empty dock, while failed sessions remain visible. Copying text gives a brief success notice without taking keyboard focus away.

In Appearance → Typography → Terminal font, choose a font installed on the browser's computer and see it applied live. The preference stays in that browser for the current Gateway, with fallback fonts when needed and bundled Nerd Font symbols for common shell prompts. See [terminal fonts](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/panels#terminal-fonts).



###### Sources and complete change list


- Send Ctrl+Space and modified F1–F12 keys correctly to interactive terminal programs. [#164688](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164688). Thanks [yetval](https://github.com/yetval) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Close a terminal tab after a successful shell exit without a signal or error, preserving failed sessions and other docked tools. [#166087](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166087). Thanks [jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Apply a browser-local terminal-font preference live and include Nerd Font symbols for shell prompts. Custom fonts must be installed locally; programming ligatures are unsupported. [#166180](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166180). Thanks [jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Preserve tabs, selection, and output when moving between bottom and right docks, without starting a replacement shell. Exited-session diagnostics are not restored after a page reload. [#166213](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166213). Thanks [jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Keep multiline terminal-table cells from unnecessarily wrapping neighboring columns. [#166549](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166549). Thanks [ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Confirm successful terminal text copies briefly while retaining typing focus. [#166669](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166669). Thanks [jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).






##### Talk and dictation


You can dictate a follow-up while the assistant replies, then choose when to send it or keep it as a draft. Starting dictation with a hold keeps recording after you release the microphone control; tap Stop separately to finish. Talk requests made after voice setup completes can reach the agent without inheriting the finished setup's cancellation.

[Talk session handling](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/talk/realtime-sessions) preserves accepted speech and tool outcomes through provider cleanup errors and avoids resending a confirmed post-call summary after a delivery-marker failure. For compatible custom realtime servers or proxies, the [OpenAI voice settings](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/voice-and-speech) accept a complete WebSocket endpoint with the OpenAI provider and Gateway relay transport, keeping API keys on the Gateway.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Dictation and spoken requests**

- Dictate a follow-up while a reply is active, then explicitly send it or retain the draft without duplicate insertion. [#166075](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166075). Thanks [jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Restore hold-to-dictate recording after microphone release, with a separate Stop tap to finish transcription. [#166843](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166843). Thanks [alfredjbclaw](https://github.com/alfredjbclaw) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Let later Talk requests outlive completed voice setup instead of immediately inheriting its cancelled state. Explicit cancellation and restart or permission boundaries still apply. [#170083](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170083). Thanks [brokemac79](https://github.com/brokemac79).
- Return the normal generic Talk failure for unusual microphone or media error names. [#166682](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166682). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

**Call records, completion, and endpoints**

- Find open calls without decoding closed-call history, share overlapping legacy requests on one connection, and avoid stale cleanup on resumed calls. [#165546](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165546). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Save accepted speech and tool outcomes through provider cleanup failures, respect the configured conversation store for summary recipients, and retry a failed delivery marker after a confirmed send without resending that summary. This does not guarantee exactly-once delivery across process restarts. [#166795](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166795). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retain completed consultation status and duration without terminal-persistence warnings, while keeping explicit cancellation effective. [#167455](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167455). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [Bartok9](https://github.com/Bartok9), and [samiralibabic](https://github.com/samiralibabic).
- Honor the selected custom Talk store and retain a committed session's identity if participant display details cannot load. [#167578](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167578). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Run Talk call records and transcript bookkeeping in the database worker, preserving committed results through cleanup failures. [#167720](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167720). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Support complete custom realtime WebSocket endpoints through `talk.realtime.providers.openai.baseUrl`, with provider `openai` and transport `gateway-relay`. Browser WebRTC, GPT-Live, and mixed Azure settings are unsupported for this option; third-party protocol compatibility still matters. [#165632](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165632). Thanks [jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson), [starship863](https://github.com/starship863), and [wangyan411303](https://github.com/wangyan411303).







#### Updates and Maintenance

Updates and Doctor address more cases where completed work or leftover recovery records prevent the next upgrade. Progress reports explain more of the waiting, and recovery guidance makes the next action easier to find.




##### Finish or repair an update


Eligible stale update records can now be settled automatically, and completed recovery records can be archived before another update begins. Managed updates also handle more cases where the Gateway was stopped but a later installation or service change was refused, restoring the preserved service when its ownership and definition can still be verified.

These changes must be present in the updater doing the work. If an older installation is already blocked, follow the [repair and recovery guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery), which may require a [manual installation into the same location](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/update-methods#alternative-manual-npm-pnpm-or-bun) first. Keep backups and stop competing package managers before recovery. Unfinished rollback, damaged packages and uncertain process ownership can still require manual attention.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Completed and abandoned update records**

- Automatically settle eligible recovery after its coordination database disappears or changes, preserve dry-run files, and archive completed records before the next update. [#169038](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169038). Public repair also handles verified candidates or newer manual installations after device-number changes. These paths do not repair unfinished rollback.
