##### Doctor migrations and repair


Doctor can recover more retained conversations, repair certain session-database problems and explain refusals more precisely. Its JSON and lint reports preserve existing database files and direct pending migrations to an explicit repair. Interactive service repairs show the proposed changes and ask separately before pausing the Gateway.

Legacy installations need the [migration guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/state-migrations). Runtime no longer discovers `.clawdbot` or `clawdbot.json` automatically. Back up first, review configuration paths, workspace locations and service runtime pins, and reconcile competing old and new roots manually. Older rollback may not follow relocated paths. Retired browser or Codex config formats require 2026.9.5 plus `openclaw doctor --fix`; retired JSON state requires 2026.9.7 Doctor on the original host. Complete these steps before upgrading further, and never use an intermediate release on databases already upgraded by a newer one.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Legacy installations and intermediate upgrades**

- Move default legacy Clawdbot path migration into Doctor and remove implicit runtime lookup of the old directory and filename. Explicit path selections are not rewritten, and competing roots require manual reconciliation. [#163376](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163376). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Require OpenClaw 2026.9.5 followed by `openclaw doctor --fix` for extension browser profiles with a nonempty saved `cdpUrl`, or provider/model API values of `openai-codex-responses`, before upgrading further. See [config migrations](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations). [#165375](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165375). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserve retired Voice Wake, binding, ACP replay and restart JSON files and require OpenClaw 2026.9.7 Doctor on the original host to migrate affected state before further upgrading. Unused retired OAuth files and the obsolete update-notification cache no longer force that step. [#165402](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165402), [#165420](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165420), [#165461](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165461). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Refuse retired runtime or referenced OAuth files before changing older database schemas or stopping the Gateway, preserving the intermediate-upgrade route. [#170051](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170051). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [bautrey](https://github.com/bautrey).
- Explain at startup that retired files were preserved for Doctor, with warnings for optional inspection failures. [#165528](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165528). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Direct saved legacy execution-approval policies to Doctor repair, tolerate malformed subagent allowlist entries, and give staged-upgrade guidance for retained pre-July sandbox registries. [#164234](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164234), [#164669](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164669). Thanks [wakqasahmed](https://github.com/wakqasahmed), [cristianoc](https://github.com/cristianoc), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Migrate legacy audit logs using a guarded hard-link fallback where supported, or retain the originals with recovery guidance. Linux and NAS migrations also handle filesystems that reject exclusive rename. [#164748](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164748), [#166048](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166048). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [bbs2i58](https://github.com/bbs2i58), [StarGazer1995](https://github.com/StarGazer1995) and [MertBasar0](https://github.com/MertBasar0).

**Conversation and stored-state recovery**

- Preserve older ACP conversation bindings during migration, and import retained metadata for a later-installed plugin without repopulating closed or replaced sessions. For restored older state, stop the Gateway, run `openclaw doctor --fix` against the same configuration and state, then restart after repair. [#162920](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162920), [#169759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169759). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).
- Migrate older flat pending-reply fields without repeatedly failing, retaining session IDs, transcripts and pending replies. [#165866](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165866). Thanks [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT).
- Reconcile leftover legacy session files while preserving newer sessions and originals, and rebuild history and search indexes for recovered conversations. [#166397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166397), [#166395](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166395). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [coolcoolwcr](https://github.com/coolcoolwcr).
- Back up and repair orphan session windows that block an agent's jobs or heartbeats. Stop the Gateway, run `openclaw doctor --fix`, and restart after repair. Unrelated damage still requires separate recovery. [#167366](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167366). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [changjunfeng8-hue](https://github.com/changjunfeng8-hue).
- Recover available retained conversations when an old import record belongs to another database, without letting that warning alone block updater restart. Missing originals still require a verified backup. [#167538](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167538). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [bipolar84](https://github.com/bipolar84).
- Retain completed archive-repair batches across interruptions, progress past empty historical session IDs, and recover interrupted archive publication. Doctor can attempt to restore a Gateway it stopped even when repair fails. A restored service does not mean the repair completed. [#165837](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165837), [#165854](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165854). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [A1fred-AI](https://github.com/A1fred-AI), [goncalonc](https://github.com/goncalonc), [mpquemel](https://github.com/mpquemel), [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [Olli0103](https://github.com/Olli0103), [h6y3](https://github.com/h6y3), [waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault) and [rauf543](https://github.com/rauf543).
- Finish eligible Codex migration when completed historical tasks can no longer deliver to their original requester, retaining task history. [#166668](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166668). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [dwonshin](https://github.com/dwonshin), [Torsie](https://github.com/Torsie), [Hensarj](https://github.com/Hensarj), [HMKR2025](https://github.com/HMKR2025), [flawedhumanoid-sudo](https://github.com/flawedhumanoid-sudo), [BsnizND](https://github.com/BsnizND), [gisk0](https://github.com/gisk0), [cortezhub](https://github.com/cortezhub), [KoennaOpenclaw](https://github.com/KoennaOpenclaw) and [juanbamorelli-source](https://github.com/juanbamorelli-source).
- Preserve malformed UTF-8 memory-file bytes rather than corrupting them during a merge, with guidance to inspect or restore the files. [#169029](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169029). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666).
- Preserve saved OAuth profiles when legacy auth files are unreadable, and replace ineffective secrets-reload advice with the active profile's Doctor repair command. [#170256](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170256), [#167650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167650). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [nosmile99](https://github.com/nosmile99), [samson1357924](https://github.com/samson1357924) and [vegisearchteam](https://github.com/vegisearchteam).
- Archive removed-account sender allowlists without reassigning their permissions, allowing later repairs to continue. [#167651](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167651). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [oshunter](https://github.com/oshunter).
- Let configured agents finish deferred startup despite stale multiple-store-selector registrations, with standalone Doctor cleanup for invalid registrations. [#170043](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170043). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [ourcoms2018](https://github.com/ourcoms2018).
- Pause automatic conversation replay during crash-loop safe mode, preserve interrupted work and resume recovery after the stable window. [#166238](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166238). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [KoalaShen](https://github.com/KoalaShen).

**Inspection, backups and diagnostics**

- Preserve SQLite database, WAL and shared-memory files during Doctor JSON and lint reporting, and avoid changing databases during migration planning. Pending migrations go to explicit repair. See [Doctor lint](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/lint). [#166857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166857), [#166671](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166671). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Recommend a compatible build or matching pre-update backup when an older build encounters newer database state, instead of misleading repair instructions. [#166906](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166906), [#166963](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166963). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Identify unreadable state databases in deep Gateway status and exit with failure. Restore only after stopping processes and following verified-backup guidance. [#167352](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167352). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Report the underlying database or filesystem cause of repair refusals, distinguish newer-schema and recovery-manifest errors, and warn when disposable inspection snapshots cannot be removed. [54cfa2f](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/54cfa2f8647c66fa34d72ad149a316caf8b8f6e4), [#164697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164697). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Complete supported older agent-database migrations before checking the final schema, while refusing damaged version-17 databases before migration can conceal missing structures. [343e56d](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/343e56d88714122b55b080a82515a1ca6ae0ee4b), [6785728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/678572811dd8b10c19ee69e2107947d099dd33c1).
- Avoid false backup rejection on Linux filesystems without reliable creation times, and preserve vector memory during backups without loading an incompatible extension on older CPUs. Vector search still needs a compatible extension. [#164363](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164363), [#165820](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165820). Thanks [jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [StevenKrebs](https://github.com/StevenKrebs), [kirylh](https://github.com/kirylh), [stunt4711](https://github.com/stunt4711) and [NovaUnboundAi](https://github.com/NovaUnboundAi).
- Skip redundant recovery copies for unchanged one-shot automations. Repairs that change state still create protected backups. [#168938](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168938). Thanks [harshitgupta31415](https://github.com/harshitgupta31415) and [wg-mojo](https://github.com/wg-mojo).
- Reduce repeated plugin-directory checks and reuse unchanged verified plugin sources across maintenance phases. [#167516](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167516), [#167535](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167535). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [palewire](https://github.com/palewire).
- Avoid repeated integrity scans of unchanged repair records and false Linux service-inspection timeouts caused by local database checks. [#168069](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168069), [#168161](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168161). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [Tosko4](https://github.com/Tosko4) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Delay pairing-storage loading until a memory-wiki check needs it, avoid redundant ownership reads inside an existing write transaction, and stop mistaking memory-reindex coordination files for held agent databases. [4bac149](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4bac1493291e7d931c0eea48b8ed07e98dc37ce0), [#166331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166331), [#166517](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166517). Thanks [sasan1200](https://github.com/sasan1200), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [noelff](https://github.com/noelff).
- Use the selected plugin generation for tool-schema checks and reuse matching provider registrations instead of registering them twice. [965ac0f](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/965ac0ffc3e8ec99a1923b48e30f27ab7391ab9b), [#166364](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166364). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [hippoley](https://github.com/hippoley).
- Explain disk-full plugin capture failures and direct users to free space and rerun Doctor. [a6496a2](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a6496a2c5d1aee637a9c7fe0381ec2fc1ce524bb).
- Recognize native Claude CLI installations outside the service PATH, and explain rejected newly entered per-agent model settings with the selected model and a recovery step. [#165273](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165273), [#168009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168009). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [mightyx](https://github.com/mightyx) and [azuretek](https://github.com/azuretek).
- Remove obsolete repair-and-restart advice after temporary agent preparation recovers, and avoid false command-cron authority warnings when no condition script is present. [#167723](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167723), [#166384](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166384). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309) and [davidchyi-beep](https://github.com/davidchyi-beep).

**Repair consent and service handling**

- Preview actual service repairs and explain remaining runtime warnings. Interactive Doctor asks before pausing the managed Gateway; declining maintenance still shows read-only findings without applying repairs. See [running Doctor](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/running). [#166950](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166950), [#166948](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166948), [#168065](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168065). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [odrobnik](https://github.com/odrobnik).
- Require a Gateway health response before calling a restart verified, with diagnostics for a running but unresponsive service. [#167713](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167713). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [CamilleValley](https://github.com/CamilleValley).
- Allow same-account settings and state repair when an app-owned Gateway is verified offline. Continue to restart or update that Gateway through OpenClaw.app. [#170768](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170768). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [shanselman](https://github.com/shanselman).
- Repair settings in an already writable externally managed state directory without changing its permissions, and wait for an exited container's lock to expire while protecting live owners. [#164990](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164990), [#165355](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165355). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [saftall](https://github.com/saftall).
- Start scheduled automations after Doctor repair without an extra Gateway restart, and avoid irrelevant Matrix migration failure on fresh installations with no Matrix settings or old data. [#165032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165032), [#165209](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165209). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Stop mistaking Doctor's own maintenance session for another owner during an authorized update. [#165417](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165417). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Clear narrowly eligible unfinished-plugin records for empty unavailable-owner work, completed official replacements or genuinely absent migration contracts, retaining stored data and real pending obligations. [#165861](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165861), [#167699](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167699). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [Franklinyung](https://github.com/Franklinyung), [ekinnee](https://github.com/ekinnee) and [ruicozibon](https://github.com/ruicozibon).
- Retain an already-verified updater relationship through later process-inspection failures and settle newly spawned children when private handoff setup fails. Unknown owners and uncertain cleanup still block. [#169563](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169563), [#169665](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169665). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Find eligible abandoned npm updater runtimes whose extra recovery-database link blocks updates. Wait for workers to exit, run Doctor from the affected installation, then retry. [#170170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170170). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [sunnyshu0925](https://github.com/sunnyshu0925) and [warhangel](https://github.com/warhangel).
- Attempt Gateway restoration after a transient resource-close failure succeeds on retry, while still reporting the cleanup error. [#169783](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169783). Thanks [romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).
- Drain retained workers before executable Doctor exits, preserving completed check history if a later crash rejects the candidate, and let the inspection worker's native resources close naturally. [#164853](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164853), [#167125](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167125). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [cunzai007-hub](https://github.com/cunzai007-hub) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).






##### Recovery copies and retained files


New recovery copies include affected older Canvas documents and Memory Core files that migrations could previously omit. Interrupted Doctor copies remain visible without hiding healthy copies beside them. An incomplete copy is still not a verified restore point, and copies created by an older updater do not gain missing files retroactively. Keep incomplete evidence for inspection and follow the [original-state capture guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery#original-state-captures).



###### Sources and complete change list


**Recovery coverage and preservation**

- Include affected legacy Canvas documents and Memory Core event logs, memory indexes and migration destinations in new recovery copies. Handle empty agent rosters and reject unsafe Canvas links before reading them. [#169436](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169436), [#169516](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169516). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Explain plugin migration backup coverage accurately and give concrete next steps when additional resources are unknown. [#169295](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169295). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Retain interrupted Doctor captures and keep adjacent healthy copies discoverable. Keep both partial and final manifest names for inspection rather than treating the pair as a usable restore point. [#166495](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166495). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [MertBasar0](https://github.com/MertBasar0) and [hippoley](https://github.com/hippoley).
- Publish pre-repair captures on supported Linux filesystems without exclusive rename, and hash natively cloned plugin files through the corrected dependency path. [#166425](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166425), [#166457](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166457). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [MertBasar0](https://github.com/MertBasar0).
- Avoid false changed-during-capture warnings when an exited database writer leaves WAL sidecars. This requires the running updater to contain the fix; later writes can still prevent automatic restoration. See [rollback and recovery](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/rollback-and-recovery). [#170351](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170351). Thanks [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Accept unchanged recovery database and journal files despite timestamp drift, while continuing to reject changed contents or replacements. [#164823](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164823). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Let repair clear eligible retained recovery after replacement of its coordination database, preserving installed files and evidence. [#164849](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164849). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [szef-svg](https://github.com/szef-svg) and [izrl613](https://github.com/izrl613).
- Apply Windows-appropriate private-directory checks in shared SQLite recovery instead of rejecting snapshots for POSIX permissions. [#169397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169397). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserve referenced historical checkpoints whose valid filenames begin with two dots, and remove only owned temporary files after a failed transcript archive write. [#164920](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164920), [#166085](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166085). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep captured Windows Bun plugin source stable after edits until an intentional reload creates a replacement instance. [#165533](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165533). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

**Copying and validation work**

- Accept unchanged plugin files when hard-link capture changes metadata, retaining checks for changed content and ownership. [#165982](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165982). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [wg-mojo](https://github.com/wg-mojo).
- Skip duplicate readiness snapshots when no startup warning needs waiting, and avoid redundant database copies when all database files are isolated inside the private validation image. [#166126](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166126), [#167451](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167451). Thanks [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [iFadi](https://github.com/iFadi), [dankarization](https://github.com/dankarization), [azuretek](https://github.com/azuretek) and [davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini).
- Reduce repeated preparation work for large plugin trees, and hash large inventories in parallel when available CPU and memory permit. Constrained hosts retain in-process hashing. [#166317](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166317), [#166741](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166741). Thanks [hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph), [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduce copying overhead for external plugins with many small dependency files, retain resource files, and initialize SSH sandbox workspaces on affected Linux volumes with clearer temporary-path errors. [#166955](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166955), [#167476](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167476). Thanks [jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen), [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [valkyriweb](https://github.com/valkyriweb) and [joequant](https://github.com/joequant).
- Avoid copying unrelated dependencies and reduce repeated package-file hashing and disk work. These changes apply when the installed updater contains them. [#168927](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168927), [#168958](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168958). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Identify changed filesystem identity and the affected coordination-database path in local refusals, with the active profile's repair command. [#167794](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167794). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault) and [masterdevill](https://github.com/masterdevill).






##### Update progress, status, and next steps


Update output names more checks before they run and preserves more of the actual failure reason, recovery warnings and follow-up commands. Reports distinguish pending maintenance, unverified readiness and failures that happened before runtime verification began. If you use `--no-restart`, keep backups until the service owner has restarted the Gateway and follow-up checks have verified it. See [update status and history](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/status-and-history).



###### Sources and complete change list


**Progress and failure explanations**

- Announce validation checks and commands before execution, and preserve refusal reasons and recovery guidance in update history, reports and Settings. [#164746](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164746), [#164782](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164782). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [coygeek](https://github.com/coygeek).
- Identify rejected recovery objects with their role, bounded filename, permissions, links and ownership information. Determine ownership before changing links or permissions. [#164858](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164858). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn), [creanet-64](https://github.com/creanet-64), [jrpasolini](https://github.com/jrpasolini), [jb168](https://github.com/jb168) and [burchk007-jpg](https://github.com/burchk007-jpg).
- Identify the installation and runtime needing attention after failed checks, and distinguish missing local Git target metadata from remote-access problems in update previews. [#166410](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166410), [#166577](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166577). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [TOM-WANG-GUANG](https://github.com/TOM-WANG-GUANG) and [Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Preserve recorded recovery warnings and paths in final console output, saved Markdown and human-readable status. Explain which updater ran checks and when permanent recovery-data cleanup should be considered. [#167509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167509), [#167802](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167802). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [GitHoubi](https://github.com/GitHoubi) and [Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Put profile-aware repair instructions first when an update fails after migrating data, and retain those instructions if safe process cleanup remains unverified even when the Gateway reports the target version. [#168905](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168905), [#170207](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170207). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [ezimerman](https://github.com/ezimerman).
- Name affected databases and backup errors in incomplete original-state capture warnings. These captures remain incomplete. [#169282](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169282). Thanks [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
- Explain optional candidate-configuration read failures while preserving the service definition, and capture included configuration without reopening migrated data. Some migration-related schema refusals can be reported as advisory. [#169284](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169284), [#170130](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170130). Thanks [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi), [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [angeliti999](https://github.com/angeliti999).
- Classify candidate-snapshot failure correctly when runtime verification never started, with the matching retry guidance. [#171457](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171457). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).

**Pending work and verification**

- Record deferred Doctor maintenance as pending, and describe recognized contention with `--no-restart` as readiness still unverified, including the service-owner restart and follow-up checks. [#167714](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167714), [#167760](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167760). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [ruicozibon](https://github.com/ruicozibon).
- Detect pending package recovery in triage even when health and update history look successful, directing operators to [update repair](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery#update-repair). [#167782](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167782). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault).
- Clear stale completed-plugin warnings, tolerate clock changes during package verification, and reconcile final summaries and saved history with completed plugin maintenance. [#169004](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169004), [#170413](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170413). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [adenzhou1350](https://github.com/adenzhou1350), [WG-Mojo](https://github.com/WG-Mojo), [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
- Preserve verified update success when publishing its result fails, and warn during repair about configured channel accounts that remain stopped. Those warnings do not restart the channels. [#169037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169037). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [kvnloo](https://github.com/kvnloo) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Treat a channel-health collection timeout alone as an update warning. Run `openclaw health` afterward because that timeout does not establish channel health. [#165852](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165852). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [vasaeru](https://github.com/vasaeru), [jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna), [rogerallen1](https://github.com/rogerallen1) and [TimofeyK](https://github.com/TimofeyK).
- Show a reduced-verification warning when large-package verification reaches resource limits and uses its fallback. Full package contents remain unverified on that path. [#164504](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164504). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Use warnings for optional configuration fallbacks, and report unknown Git ahead/behind counts when shallow history cannot support an exact comparison. Unknown does not mean up to date. [#165539](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165539), [#165316](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165316). Thanks [Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen) and [ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii).

**Diagnostic and output handling**

- Keep optional progress logging from aborting an update when state storage is busy, and tolerate temporary progress-output congestion during database inventory. Required recovery records and real I/O failures still matter. [#168210](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168210), [#168493](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168493). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna), [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [creanet-64](https://github.com/creanet-64).
- Honor explicitly selected `TMPDIR`, `TMP` or `TEMP` values over managed-service defaults for update snapshot placement. [#165830](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165830). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
- Identify the background helper responsible for model calls in CLI diagnostics, helping investigate usage. [#164140](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164140). Thanks [dasx](https://github.com/dasx) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).






##### Shutdown and maintenance cleanup


Shutdown logs identify slow cleanup steps, queued heartbeats no longer keep an otherwise idle Gateway open, and stalled transcript indexing gets a five-second grace before cancellation. Saved transcripts remain available after restart, with unfinished indexing rediscovered later. Required writes and cleanup can still take longer, so five seconds is not a total shutdown deadline. See [restart recovery](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery).



###### Sources and complete change list


**Shutdown and worker cleanup**

- Name slow shutdown steps in default logs and list unfinished steps when the deadline is reached. [#165670](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165670). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Stop queued heartbeats holding an idle shutdown open, while keeping the grace period for active replies and terminal writes. [#167666](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167666). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Cancel stalled transcript indexing after a five-second grace while retaining accepted writes, worker exit and database cleanup. Committed history remains available. [#170493](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170493). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) and [O96a](https://github.com/O96a).
- Allow terminated POSIX child processes longer to disappear before Doctor or update reports uncertain cleanup. [#171497](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171497). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Let cooperative Gateway suspension wait for brief pending writes before deciding deployment remains blocked. [#167184](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167184). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Report WhatsApp QR startup deadlines without an unhandled rejection, and clear the timer when worker reconnect preparation fails. [#165340](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165340). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).






##### Startup maintenance


After a multi-agent restart, smaller agents can become available while larger conversation histories are still preparing. The Gateway can also serve clients while optional maintenance continues after readiness, and startup logs show more detail about where time is spent. Required configuration, database and permission checks still run before admission.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Agent preparation and optional work**

- Prepare agents independently so smaller agents can become available while larger histories are still loading, with progress warnings for agents still waiting. [#165107](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165107). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Let smaller conversation-history rebuilds finish ahead of an earlier agent's large backlog by yielding between completed sessions. [#165207](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165207). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Move optional history repair and maintenance after readiness, add more detailed startup timings, skip unnecessary plugin repair checks and use a consistent snapshot for recovery inspection. See [logging](https://docs.openclaw.ai/logging). [#165218](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165218), [#165498](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165498). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).







#### Messaging

Messaging changes cover questions that need your answer, progress while work continues, and replies with files or generated media. Channel-specific updates below also explain setup changes and upgrade steps that may affect waiting messages or access.




##### Replies, follow-ups, and progress


Telegram and Discord can show the current operation and delegated-task status without detailed tool logs. Discord now enables this progress view when no streaming mode is set. You can still turn it off or choose an answer-preview mode. When background media work is handed back to the conversation, its progress card stays visible until the results arrive. Generation or delivery failures remain visible. See [progress drafts](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/progress-drafts).

Routed agent conversations now receive the [question the agent is waiting for](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/ask-user), so you can answer where you started the request. Automatic delivery of the final answer remains a separate setting. Follow-ups also wait behind image completion and delivery instead of failing because the conversation changed during preparation.

Long replies preserve more readable chunks, emoji, accents and code examples. History keeps quotes and tool status, while delivered messages shed false delivery warnings. An accepted request or queue acknowledgment still does not establish that its recipient received a reply.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Improvements**

- Opted-in plugin tools can deliver a finished reply without another model response. Codex tools also require the direct-only catalog setting. [#164558](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164558). Thanks [mbelinky](https://github.com/mbelinky). See [tool plugins](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/tool-plugins).
- Exact-run delivery-history lookups use improved database maintenance. [#165085](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165085). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Default logs explain failed steering and whether follow-up input was accepted, rejected or dropped. [#165876](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165876). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [PilonQV](https://github.com/PilonQV) and [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Open chats reuse unchanged command lists and model choices during ordinary conversation activity, reducing repeated metadata requests. [#166250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166250). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Formatting long replies requires less processing when splitting text across messaging channels. [#166493](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166493). Thanks [boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Telegram and Discord show operation and delegated-task progress with detailed logs disabled. Discord defaults to progress when its streaming mode is unset. Cards may become stale after restart. [#166861](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166861). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [samiralibabic](https://github.com/samiralibabic) and [dankarization](https://github.com/dankarization).
- Successful emoji reactions no longer add redundant Message rows to Slack progress cards. Failed or uncertain outcomes remain visible. [#166880](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166880). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [notmnp](https://github.com/notmnp).
- Telegram and Discord keep media progress cards until results handed back by background tasks arrive, retaining generation or delivery failures. [#167362](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167362). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Renaming, archiving or changing a conversation's model avoids scanning unrelated saved prompts during restart-recovery cleanup. [#167643](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167643). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

**Bug fixes**

- Streamed text avoids a tiny trailing message at an exact size boundary. [0427411](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/04274119f651594b74d581ae452dc8ab36d30889).
- Accepted messages start after worker setup instead of failing during runtime selection. [7ecc80a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7ecc80a457366091dd61448bcc319cf1a82a5da9).
- Discord's editable preview includes the last character of a complete batch without an unnecessary wait. [9df0cd0](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9df0cd095766ff05864aa67aa91b627a347a4d1a).
- Preparing long, mostly ASCII reply chunks uses less CPU. [#163573](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163573). Thanks [boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Quotes load with conversation history, and navigation to older quoted messages waits for their rows to load. [#164605](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164605). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Replies containing carriage returns keep internal attachment directives out of displayed text. [#164657](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164657). Thanks [ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Loaded history retains tool status, and progress listeners remain attached to their owning Gateway after a client disconnects. [#164848](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164848). Thanks [sjf-oa](https://github.com/sjf-oa) and [sjf](https://github.com/sjf).
- Embedded agents sending between conversations are told when a result was saved only in the transcript without external-channel delivery. [#164860](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164860). Thanks [jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [Jackten](https://github.com/Jackten).
- A stalled plugin shutdown no longer changes a successful message command into exit code 13. Original send failures remain failures. [#164915](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164915). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Recovered and internal-session progress attachments survive scratch-file cleanup and reload. Already-lost files are not restored. [#165275](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165275). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Slack and Discord source-conversation header buttons are retired, with old button metadata removed while transcripts remain. [#165334](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165334). Thanks [Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Injected reports and alerts appear once as standalone notes without ending the active reply. Intentionally repeated identical notes remain separate. [#165549](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165549). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [legarconc](https://github.com/legarconc).
- Incoming messages defer during startup inspection so their transport can retry, with membership refreshed before effects begin. [#165738](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165738). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Chat sends tolerate activity summaries or the preceding turn updating the same conversation's metadata during preparation. [#166027](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166027). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Emoji and combining accents that fit within a reply block stay together when streamed prose is split. [#166567](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166567). Thanks [rudycelekli](https://github.com/rudycelekli) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Follow-up instructions wait behind image completion and delivery instead of receiving a changed-session error. Waiting work remains cancellable. [#166597](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166597). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Delivery observers receive missing send notifications when fallback queue acknowledgment settles a partially failed batch. Failure notifications still report failure. [#166659](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166659). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Identical Codex ultrafast policy warnings normally appear once per stored chat across turns and reconnects. New or reset chats and changed warnings still notify. [#167220](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167220). Thanks [jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Plain-text replies retain command placeholders and generic type examples, including Telegram model and thinking help. [#167953](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167953). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Long formatted code replies fill chunks more effectively, avoiding unnecessary tiny messages while preserving code formatting and internal whitespace. [#167958](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167958). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Enabled persistent Thinking messages arrive before streamed Telegram and Discord answers without duplicate reasoning. [#168062](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168062). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Delivered messages lose false sidebar and Inbox warnings even when an earlier send remains unconfirmed. Uncertain sends are not replayed. [#168090](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168090). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Routed agent turns send their blocking question to the originating messaging conversation. Final-answer delivery remains separately configured. [#170039](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170039). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).






##### Telegram


Telegram preserves waiting questions when you use settings or skill commands. Ordinary commands wait behind pending input, while authorized Stop, `/new` and `/reset` still cancel it. Streaming previews stay visible while a replacement is delayed, and explicit reply targets are respected even when implicit threading is off. See [Telegram messaging](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/telegram/messaging).

Progress cards can stay visible while delegated work continues, including when the parent resumes or delegates again. These cards live in the running process, so a restart can leave an old card behind. Requested verbose summaries and detailed tool icons are also restored. Optional [rich messages](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/telegram/rich-messages) remain off by default and now preserve table values and placement.

**When upgrading from the legacy disk spool**, the first import retires instructions older than 24 hours, including instructions that genuinely never ran. Their source files and receipts remain retained, but those entries cannot be resubmitted. Ask the sender to resend any work still needed. Rows imported before this change remain unchanged. See [Telegram transports](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/telegram/transports).



###### Sources and complete change list


**Improvements**

- Telegram progress cards remain visible through delegated work, parent resumption and further delegation. Restart persistence is not provided. [#166101](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166101). Thanks [Kimiyu-186](https://github.com/Kimiyu-186), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Detailed Telegram tool progress regains recognizable tool emoji in ordinary and retained cards. The existing tool-progress preference still controls these logs. [#167392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167392). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Incoming GIFs and animations arrive as video attachments with any caption. Existing file-size limits and video-model requirements still apply. [#168011](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168011). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [ThisisPeggy](https://github.com/ThisisPeggy), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [manus-pi](https://github.com/manus-pi).
- First-time legacy-spool imports retire instructions older than 24 hours, including unprocessed work. Previously imported rows stay unchanged. [#170068](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170068). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [angeliti999](https://github.com/angeliti999).

**Bug fixes**

- Inbound polling resolves its worker in source and captured-plugin installations and both supported compiled layouts. [#154286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154286). Thanks [xydt-juyaohui](https://github.com/xydt-juyaohui), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [markfietje](https://github.com/markfietje).
- Node-command completions and delivery-error follow-ups retain the account, conversation and topic that started the command. [#160675](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160675). Thanks [brokemac79](https://github.com/brokemac79).
- Streaming previews stay visible until a replacement is accepted, rather than disappearing while replacement delivery waits or fails. [#164649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164649). Thanks [jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [jmar5439](https://github.com/jmar5439).
- Preview edits recover from temporary server errors without leaving a stale partial message beside a duplicate final reply. Retry limits still apply. [#165072](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165072). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Message-tool sends, including inline buttons, work after chat handoffs without an invalid inferred reply target. [#165137](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165137). Thanks [mbelinky](https://github.com/mbelinky).
- Replies rejected before sending show an accurate failure notice without misleading recovery warnings. Ownership or binding problems still need correction. [#167470](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167470). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [mgr-nick](https://github.com/mgr-nick).
- Polls, stickers, topics and reactions honor authorization withdrawn during lookup, and closing the transport stops another queued retry. [#167635](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167635). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Streamed answers honor explicit reply targets by replacing previews attached to a different message. Threading defaults are unchanged. [#167895](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167895). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Settings and skill commands preserve waiting questions. Ordinary commands queue behind pending input; authorized Stop, `/new` and `/reset` still cancel. [#168004](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168004). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [vicary](https://github.com/vicary).
- `/verbose on` restores requested standalone tool summaries in default progress mode; `/verbose full` also restores completed output and permitted details. [#168016](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168016). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Opted-in rich replies preserve table values and their position before quotes or lists. [#168383](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168383). Thanks [dhruv1220](https://github.com/dhruv1220), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [barry-ssb](https://github.com/barry-ssb).






##### Discord


New corrections and cancellations can reach reply processing while an earlier deferred follow-up waits. Your configured queue mode still determines how that input is handled, so this does not promise immediate interruption. Streaming previews also preserve paragraph breaks and code blocks. See [Discord messaging](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/discord/messaging).

Doctor preserves Discord owner DM destinations used by heartbeats. If an earlier migration stripped the user type, Doctor can recover it from safely matching retained configuration backups; otherwise the owner must correct it manually. Specialized voice and execution-approver selectors may still reject typed entries. Detailed tool logs remain optional and off by default.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Improvements**

- Optional Discord tool-progress logs show tool-specific icons. [#168032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168032). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

**Bug fixes**

- New Discord corrections and cancellations can reach processing while a deferred follow-up waits, subject to the selected queue mode. [#159265](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159265). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [aatreya](https://github.com/aatreya).
- Streaming previews preserve paragraph breaks and continuous code blocks across chunk boundaries. [#165120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165120). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Health inspection avoids loading Discord voice and activity code that could delay Gateway connections and approval workflows. [#166736](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166736). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Doctor preserves owner heartbeat DM targets and can recover stripped user types from matching configuration backups. Unrecoverable targets need manual correction. [#167059](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167059). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [jaiden0](https://github.com/jaiden0). See [channel ownership during updates](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations#channel-ownership-during-an-update).






##### Slack


Slack native streaming places later progress and answers below your new follow-up message, with Stop still available. Short bare room names such as `general` resolve correctly. Longer names beginning with c or g, such as `community`, still need the `#community` form.

Membership checks now use a fixed 60-second cache. `OPENCLAW_SLACK_CHANNEL_MEMBERS_CACHE_TTL_MS` no longer changes that interval, including when it was set to zero, so membership changes may remain cached during that window. Logging changes explain skipped mentions and remove unrelated heartbeat warnings.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Improvements**

- Normal logs explain when a mention is skipped because Slack's conversation type could not be resolved. [#157953](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157953). Thanks [Olli0103](https://github.com/Olli0103), [shannon0430](https://github.com/shannon0430) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Membership caching uses a fixed 60-second expiry and removes the former environment override, including its zero-cache setting. [#165330](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165330). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

**Bug fixes**

- Short bare channel names resolve to actual rooms, restoring affected allowlist routing and replies. Longer c/g-prefixed names still need `#`. [#155882](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155882). Thanks [MertBasar0](https://github.com/MertBasar0), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [jared-rebel](https://github.com/jared-rebel).
- Native streaming keeps later progress and answers below a new human follow-up, preserving Stop. [#163511](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163511). Thanks [Olli0103](https://github.com/Olli0103), [Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen), [leovoloshin](https://github.com/leovoloshin) and [Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007).
- Socket Mode stops flooding Gateway logs with two unrelated heartbeat diagnostic warnings. [#164305](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164305). Thanks [jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309) and [Johannes-Berggren](https://github.com/Johannes-Berggren).
- Long Slack and IRC replies keep ordinary emoji and accented characters together at message boundaries when the character fits the allowed chunk. [#167025](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167025). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).






##### WhatsApp setup


Closing or replacing channel setup now stops an outdated wizard from starting WhatsApp QR pairing after you have moved on.

##### Feishu


Feishu quoted messages, fetched threads and forwarded text show readable mentions. Streaming replies skip outdated waiting previews before the completed answer, and overlapping settings reloads no longer let an old connection's cleanup stop DM replies.

Rejected document and Drive requests retain Feishu's error explanation and request log ID to help diagnose the refusal. Permissions and token recovery still need their existing remedies. See [Feishu](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/feishu).

##### Matrix, IRC, LINE, Tlon, and other channels


Matrix now keeps a valid recovery key after authenticated verification, even if it cannot yet use a backup. A retained key alone does not guarantee a backup or restored messages. Recovery also refuses corrupted UTF-8 messages, and invite-allowlist warnings explain why a user ID cannot match a room. Use room IDs or aliases for that list. See [Matrix](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/matrix).

IRC fixes preserve long multilingual replies, literal backslashes and server passwords with spaces or a leading colon. Oversized incoming records now disconnect the peer, with reconnect backoff reaching 30 seconds. LINE rejects malformed question-button replies without starting an unintended new turn, and Mattermost stops retrying permanent refusals while retaining retries for temporary failures.

Tlon club messages can no longer use a claimed author identity to gain owner commands, administrative or approval actions, or elevated ship and name permissions. Ordinary allowlisted club messages can still start work or receive private replies under a claimed ship identity. If a pending DM was saved before this upgrade without an origin marker, approving it grants access but does not replay its saved message. This also applies to genuine one-to-one requests. The sender must resend the message. Tlon links and images also preserve addresses with nested parentheses and work within more formatted messages.

Nextcloud Talk private-network operations use `network.dangerouslyAllowPrivateNetwork`. Retired flat aliases no longer grant access. If your private server depended on one, follow [Doctor's configuration migration guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations), including the intermediate-release recovery route where required.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Improvements**

- Matrix warns about invite-allowlist entries that cannot match a room. Sender rules still belong in their separate sender allowlists. [#161607](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161607). Thanks [xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn), [captain-marlow](https://github.com/captain-marlow) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Tlon sends native links embedded in prose and native images inside formatted messages, retaining surrounding formatting. Image-containing lists still use paragraph fallback. [#164828](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164828). Thanks [jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [yetval](https://github.com/yetval) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- IRC disconnects oversized incoming records at its fixed limit and backs off reconnects by up to 30 seconds. [#165018](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165018). Thanks [eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).

**Bug fixes**

- Tlon preserves complete link and image URLs containing balanced parentheses. [193f7e6](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/193f7e62a22fe7f752741d2a8c65767ffbf6cd23).
- Matrix refuses malformed UTF-8 during saved-message recovery instead of sending silently altered text. [#133338](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133338). Thanks [RileyJJY](https://github.com/RileyJJY) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Malformed LINE question-button replies no longer start unintended agent turns; valid choices still answer the waiting question. [#158694](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158694). Thanks [Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Long IRC replies allow for the server's forwarding prefix so CJK text and emoji are not partly lost. [#159730](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159730). Thanks [rodricksz4h5](https://github.com/rodricksz4h5) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Nextcloud Talk consistently uses the documented nested private-network permission. Retired flat aliases no longer grant access. [#164258](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164258). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Matrix accepts and retains an authenticated valid recovery key even when verification does not newly enable backup use. Wrong and rotated keys remain rejected. [#165236](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165236). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- IRC preserves literal backslashes in messages, NickServ passwords and valid recipients while filtering actual control characters. [#165719](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165719). Thanks [Sedrak-Hovhannisyan](https://github.com/Sedrak-Hovhannisyan) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- IRC sends server passwords containing spaces or a leading colon as one complete login parameter. [#165721](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165721). Thanks [Sedrak-Hovhannisyan](https://github.com/Sedrak-Hovhannisyan) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Tlon club author claims cannot inherit owner, administrative, approval or elevated ship/name permissions. Pre-upgrade pending DMs without origin markers require a resend after approval. [#165801](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165801). Thanks [eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
- New Tlon messages preserve complete link and image addresses containing balanced or nested parentheses. Existing messages are unchanged. [#167191](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167191). Thanks [ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Mattermost stops retrying permanent request refusals even when their text mentions rate limits or server failures. Transient failures still retry. [#167244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167244). Thanks [Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).






##### Attachments and generated media


Conversations can continue and reopen after corrupt images are excluded from model requests, when an image backend is available. Stored originals remain untouched. Photos sent through WhatsApp's document picker or LINE's file picker can be recognized from their actual bytes and passed to an already vision-capable model; genuine documents remain documents.

Media requested in WebChat keeps its results, text and completion updates in that chat instead of following an old external-channel route. Explicit external destinations remain supported. Attachment updates also preserve filenames, recognize more text encodings, and keep code examples readable in web-fetch text output. See [image generation](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/image-generation) and [media understanding](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/media-understanding).

If a custom transcriber rejects an empty language argument, omit both the language flag and its value to use autodetection. Unset placeholders still become empty arguments. Link previews now read a bounded page head, so unusually late metadata may be omitted.

##### X mentions and replies


The opt-in [X channel](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/x) lets a configured bot receive approved public mentions and answer in public text reply chains. Setup requires a confidential OAuth app, bot credentials and suitable paid X API access. DMs, original posts, likes, follows and media uploads are outside this channel's supported actions. Streaming and periodic backfill supplement polling without guaranteeing delivery or response time; backfill adds paid API reads.

[API-spend budgets](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/x#costs) default to **$100 per UTC day and $1,000 per cycle**, with the cycle starting on day 1. These defaults also apply to existing accounts without overrides. Zero blocks paid work, and there is no unlimited setting. Estimates are separate from model costs and X's billing caps, may overcount, and can be exceeded by delivered stream bursts. They are not a hard invoice ceiling.

[Guest repository replies](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/x#guest-mode) remain off by default. They require a deliberately selected repository workspace, workspace-only file access, no skills and the documented sandbox-off setup. Guests get read/list access and, on capable hosts, restricted hidden helpers for the same agent. The operator must choose the correct repository; the plugin does not verify it. Defaults allow five admitted mentions per author, account and UTC day, with ten context posts; excess mentions silently drop. This quota does not control API spending.

Guest setups must choose `followup` or `collect` for `messages.queue.byChannel.x`, or use a safe global queue mode. The unset default is `steer`, which blocks guests, as does `interrupt`. Existing guest users may need to change this setting. Maintainer mentions keep their normal tools.

Optional [GitHub collaborator-derived access](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/x#verify-github-collaborators) requires credentials to list collaborators and paid X handle lookups. It trusts collaborators' declared X handles without proving handle ownership. Successful synchronization removes obsolete derived grants, while failed synchronization retains the last successful grants. Removing a stored X grant now stops work still being authorized; separate configured permissions still apply, and dispatched work or sent replies cannot be undone.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Improvements**

- Configured X bots receive approved public mentions and answer in public text reply chains using available conversation context. Paid API access is required. [#165138](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165138). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Configurable X API budgets and usage estimates pause unaffordable polling or replies and retain context for later processing. Existing accounts inherit defaults without overrides. [#165351](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165351). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Administrators can enable repository-question guest replies with isolated sessions and restricted read/list access, retaining normal tools for maintainers. [#165354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165354). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Streaming periodically checks for missed mentions and reports repeated unparseable events without exposing their content. The added backfill reads are priced. [#165376](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165376). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Capable hosts can use hidden same-agent helpers for X repository assistance within guest restrictions, without writes, execution or cross-agent access. [#165543](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165543). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Opt-in synchronization derives maintainer access from eligible GitHub collaborators' declared X handles and binds resolved numeric identities through handle reuse. Failed syncs retain stale grants. [#168071](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168071). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

**Bug fixes**

- Removing stored X permission applies to mentions and public replies still being authorized, preventing stale grants from dispatching new work. [#165244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165244). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mention streaming uses the bot's user token to create subscriptions, accepts documented Activity events and explains access failures when falling back to polling. Required access and tokens still apply. [#165337](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165337). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Plugin-channel queue overrides accept channel IDs. X guest replies require `followup` or `collect`; unset `steer` and explicit `interrupt` block them. [#165965](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165965). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).







#### Memory

Memory search refreshes after provider changes, and long conversations gain smaller summary requests and provider checkpoints while keeping the saved transcript.




##### Memory search and indexing


[EmbeddingGemma memory search](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/memory-search) now formats questions and saved notes for their different retrieval roles. Older indexes rebuild automatically on the next search or sync, so the first pass does extra embedding work. Remove any proxy workaround that already adds those prefixes to avoid adding them twice.

Managed [llama.cpp embeddings](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/llama-cpp) use smaller default memory allocations and handle ordinary chunks that failed with custom models or older EmbeddingGemma paths. Explicit tuning is preserved, but the lower default concurrency trades throughput for memory, and unusually long requests may need larger native limits. With Memory Core selected and file watching enabled, new notes also keep reaching search after provider or plugin reloads.

**Search and recall**

Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [polley-mister](https://github.com/polley-mister), [jared-rebel](https://github.com/jared-rebel), [ndakota79](https://github.com/ndakota79), and [h6y3](https://github.com/h6y3).
