##### Memory learning, Dream Diary, and Wiki


Automatic memory saving before compaction stays within its designated memory tools, preventing the saving turn from regaining unrelated project tools. Daily note saving also rejects exact copies of already-saved blocks. [Dream Diary](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/dreaming) entries can take longer than a minute when the existing agent and provider timeouts allow it, instead of falling back solely because of the old fixed cutoff. Diary entries describe background work and do not themselves promote information into memory.

[Memory Wiki](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/memory-wiki) reuses valid caches across CLI processes and finds multi-word matches across page metadata. Searches respond to cancellation and have a 30-second deadline, although work already underway can take longer to stop. Memory tools also remain available through routine session bookkeeping, with clearer guidance when an old parent session prevents access.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Memory saving and access**

- Keep automatic memory-saving turns restricted to their designated tools [#166650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166650). Thanks [jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reject exact copied blocks during daily note saving and allow a retry containing new entries [#168123](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168123). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [bit-777](https://github.com/bit-777), and [touraround](https://github.com/touraround).
- Preserve memory access through unrelated session activity and routine metadata changes [#166419](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166419). Thanks [jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Keep memory access checks working while long conversations save routine bookkeeping [#166758](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166758). Thanks [jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Explain stale-parent memory refusals and the need to respawn a helper from a current session [#166422](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166422). Thanks [jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Recognize scheduled-run memories through cyclic ancestry and references to pruned parents [#165601](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165601). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Stop maintaining a redundant memory database index during writable initialization [#165075](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165075). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

**Dreaming and Wiki**

- Let slow Dream Diary requests use the existing agent timeout, subject to the provider timeout [#167984](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167984). Longer runs can occupy background capacity longer. Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [woshicby](https://github.com/woshicby), [pfrederiksen](https://github.com/pfrederiksen), [paterskip](https://github.com/paterskip), [Kaspnov](https://github.com/Kaspnov), and [SWDadmin](https://github.com/SWDadmin).
- Wait for agent preparation before cleaning up historical dreaming artifacts after restart [#167722](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167722). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep scheduled consolidation and pending Dream Diary writes working across plugin reloads [#167724](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167724). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuse valid unchanged Wiki caches created by a separate CLI process [#167929](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167929). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).
- Match Wiki query words across compiled page metadata and avoid unnecessary page reads once enough results are found [#166355](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166355). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).
- Stop Wiki searches on cancellation or after their deadline with a tool error [#166329](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166329). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).






##### Saved conversation history


[Chat history](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/webchat) loads in smaller pages and stops collecting older messages once a page is full, with earlier messages still available through pagination. Concurrent reads can use additional readers, while overlapping refreshes share repeated work. Follow-up messages also receive a neutral notice when an older image is no longer available in the current context, without being told that the earlier image inspection failed.



###### Sources and complete change list


**History loading and recovery**

- Load smaller history pages and avoid repeatedly downloading unchanged history [#165897](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165897). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Stop older-history reads when the response page is full while retaining pagination [#167032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167032). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Allow warm concurrent history and model-context reads to use additional database readers [#167029](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167029). Additional readers use memory and can increase the work in an initial burst. Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Share database work between overlapping session-list refreshes [d60b095](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d60b0950a2d911da471c00fdb1648f7c5d61bd09).
- Avoid a history-read ownership failure during concurrent reply startup [20ef748](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/20ef7487dd5e1267101689eeca8ab04f0944fe9b).
- Use current saved history for replay and reply delivery, and keep meeting exports consistent during concurrent edits [66471ca](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/66471cad80ee9fdef9dacff66319c008543026db). Export staging briefly needs space for one additional copy of the exported artifact.
- Keep eligible read-only history recovery available while writable startup is pending [a73ef55](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a73ef559b23a6325f0ea39cc90dac22eaf50a916).
- Release extra history-discovery read ownership that could obstruct later recovery [7294c69](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7294c691f2ce7a0a26191faff4a44cf32a3212bb).
- Avoid false history-read failures when queued writes already finished before the read began [ab56112](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/ab561129c2dcf0dcbea60037ed9f6d30d78eff90).

**Images and access checks**

- Describe unavailable older images as missing historical context [eb1b48c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/eb1b48c58b61dec3cc273587d44e4f9a7eaa29f9) and remove instructions that told follow-ups to deny earlier image inspection [#166165](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166165). The original image file is not restored. Thanks [jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [nogikeyaksh](https://github.com/nogikeyaksh).
- Refuse anchored history belonging to a different conversation when aliases collide [#164512](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164512). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Recheck access and session identity before manual history trimming restores archived messages [#166506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166506). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).






##### Long conversations and compaction


[Compaction](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/compaction) now summarizes a bounded sample of oversized histories instead of exhaustively processing every older message. It prioritizes recent exchanges and earlier requests, decisions, and corrections, reducing the work sent to the summarizer. The full saved transcript remains intact, but older details can be absent from the summary. Qualifying automatic-summary timeouts can also continue with recent context, so resend an older detail if it is essential to the next answer.

Eligible direct OpenAI Responses and Anthropic API-key conversations use provider checkpoints by default. ChatGPT sign-in, Anthropic subscriptions, and intermediary routes are outside this change. Focused OpenAI compaction and endpoint failures retain local summarization, and the next message waits for saved history to become readable after native compaction. Provider compaction can consume substantially more input tokens, so it does not guarantee lower cost. You can opt out with `responsesCompactEndpoint: false` or `anthropicServerCompaction: false` in the model parameters described in the [OpenAI](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/advanced) and [Anthropic](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/anthropic) guidance.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Summary input and provider checkpoints**

- Bound built-in summary input with a prioritized conversation sample while retaining the full saved transcript [#167916](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167916). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [elharonyclaw](https://github.com/elharonyclaw), and [wynxo](https://github.com/wynxo).
- Enable provider compaction on eligible direct API-key routes, with opt-outs and local fallback [#168164](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168164). A rejected Anthropic checkpoint can still fail that turn; the next turn uses saved history. Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Wait for readable saved history before the next message after native compaction [#169583](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169583). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Check provider-compacted history against the complete request budget and fall back locally when it is too large [#167900](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167900). A message too large by itself receives guidance to shorten it or choose a larger-context model. Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Leave automatic Claude CLI compaction to Claude Code without requiring a separate Anthropic API key [#162066](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162066). Thanks [rogeriochaves](https://github.com/rogeriochaves), [llmsommelier](https://github.com/llmsommelier), [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [ajmtrz](https://github.com/ajmtrz), [MaShAn1027](https://github.com/MaShAn1027), [Jeehut](https://github.com/Jeehut), and [Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).
- Avoid redundant host compaction after native compaction in affected Codex sessions with a configured transcript-byte limit [#168051](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168051). Thanks [ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn), and [robertoronderosjr](https://github.com/robertoronderosjr).
- Incorporate recent corrections and completed checks into the main continuation summary [#167757](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167757). Thanks [jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Preserve completed exchanges in durable context engines after a user steers a running reply [#168394](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168394). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [robertoronderosjr](https://github.com/robertoronderosjr).

**Continuation and recovery**

- Let streaming summaries continue while producing output, with an inactivity limit and a bounded total run [#164263](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164263). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [elharonyclaw](https://github.com/elharonyclaw), [liyangbing](https://github.com/liyangbing), [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc), and [wynxo](https://github.com/wynxo).
- Continue after qualifying automatic-summary timeouts using recent context instead of another summary request [#164246](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164246). Older unsummarized facts leave the active context. Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Fall back to recent whole turns when a configured transcript-byte limit cannot be met through compaction, with a notice to resend essential details [#167505](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167505). Saved history is preserved. Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [jaikharbanda](https://github.com/jaikharbanda), and [jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna).
- Run required compaction before helper-completion and approval-follow-up replies while preserving the selected model [#163117](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163117). Thanks [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [AXEG0](https://github.com/AXEG0), and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Give an active answer up to 60 seconds to finish before manual `/compact` interrupts it, then ask for a resend when cancellation succeeds [#166163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166163). Cancellation can take another 15 seconds to settle; tool actions are not undone or automatically replayed. Thanks [holny](https://github.com/holny), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [alexph-dev](https://github.com/alexph-dev).
- Use a healthy configured fallback for compaction during primary credential cooldown, preserving explicit model choices [#166459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166459). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [hawkit](https://github.com/hawkit).
- Give corrective retries their recovery window after a long response reaches its output limit [#167708](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167708). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserve default memory excerpt limits when an agent overrides only one limit [#164633](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164633). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Report recognized prompt-size rejections with advice to shorten the prompt or select a larger-context model [#159712](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159712). Thanks [sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng), [NovaUnboundAi](https://github.com/NovaUnboundAi), and [altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
- Attempt context recovery or give a clear context error instead of shrinking an OpenAI-compatible answer allowance to almost nothing [#167878](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167878). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson), [amittell](https://github.com/amittell), [glebsergeevich](https://github.com/glebsergeevich), and [dankarization](https://github.com/dankarization).

**Context preparation and guidance**

- Read session and transcript statistics for compaction through background workers [#165406](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165406). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

Thanks [jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman), [Clode2026](https://github.com/Clode2026), and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

#### Skills

Skill Workshop puts learned procedures, saved versions and learning controls together so you can see what your agent has learned and change it.




##### Learn skills with saved versions and Undo


Agents can save repeatable procedures directly in [Skill Workshop](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/skill-workshop). You can read their instructions, inspect support files and compare saved versions before restoring them, while chat notices offer Undo. Learned skills stay available to their owning agent despite skill allowlists or toggles, so archive a skill to hide it from that agent.

Automatic [self-learning](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/self-learning) can send conversation and tool content to your configured model provider and incur extra model charges. Choose Learning Off to stop background review and unused-skill cleanup; explicit `/learn` remains available.

When upgrading older Workshop settings, existing automatic learning or an unset mode stays automatic, while retired `propose` and legacy enabled-only settings become `off`. Settings already migrated to `off` need an explicit change to re-enable automatic learning. Doctor preserves old pending or quarantined drafts for export instead of activating them. Keep a pre-upgrade backup if you need to return to the old proposal-based installation.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Learning and recovery**

- Direct learning replaces the proposal queue, with archive, restore and Undo. Workshop keeps ten versions per skill and 500 recent changes per agent, so older Undo targets can expire; new-skill Undo archives it. Automatic cleanup can archive skills after 30 unused days only with reliable embedded, unsandboxed tracking, excluding Codex and preserving skills named by cron jobs. [#161057](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161057) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Learning guidance focuses skills on repeatable procedures, with personal facts directed to memory and repository knowledge to repository documentation. [#167404](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167404) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Active and archived skills, readable instructions, support files and per-skill history share one Workshop view. Retained versions remain accessible after their entries leave the recent-change feed; large comparisons show the saved instructions when a line comparison is too large. [#167440](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167440) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

The retired proposal hooks have no before-save replacement. Plugin authors should follow the [Workshop hook migration guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/removed-surfaces#skill-workshop-proposal-hooks).

**Settings and bug fixes**

- The combined migration preserves automatic learning while removing obsolete proposal settings. Retired `propose` still becomes `off`, and existing `off` stays off. [#167641](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167641), [#167846](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167846) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Configuration-load failures show an error and Retry button while the skill library remains usable. [#167656](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167656) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Compare shows a saved version's own description and other descriptive changes, including when its instructions have not changed. [#168001](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168001) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).






##### Find and load skills


After installing a skill's required command into a location already visible to OpenClaw, start a new conversation to use it without restarting. Saved conversations keep their selected skill sources when another skill with the same name becomes available. Larger [skill libraries](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/skills) also avoid repeated metadata reads and can share search indexes across concurrent runs.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Discovery and availability**

- Gateway clients can browse and search ClawHub plugins and skills, filter official listings and show installation status for the selected agent. Keyword searches are bounded by upstream results, and changed results can require restarting pagination. [#163459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163459) Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- New conversations recheck installed prerequisites, while saved conversations preserve their selected local or remote skill sources. [#165529](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165529), [3f9636b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3f9636b36ec742c31382e9fc4db553c21c55c5cf). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Unavailable-skill messages explain that a skill is unavailable to the current agent and suggest searching its available skills. [#164884](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164884) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Descriptions and metadata wrapped across multiple lines in a skill's configuration header retain their complete values. [#165531](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165531) Thanks [@SashaMIT](https://github.com/SashaMIT).
- Chat replies retain their authored text when ClawHub recommendations return no results. [#166452](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166452) Thanks [@a6xa3](https://github.com/a6xa3) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

**Large libraries and background work**

- Large skill libraries and new-conversation defaults avoid repeated metadata reads and unnecessary avatar copying. [#165106](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165106) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Concurrent runs searching identical installed skill instructions can share a search index and use less memory. [#165173](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165173) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Watched skill collections avoid rescanning unchanged directories and repeating the same path warnings during unrelated refreshes. Files outside watched locations may need a manual refresh or restart to appear. [#165304](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165304) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session suggestion lists reuse prepared conversation information, reducing repeated work when loading large histories. [#165570](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165570) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Watching large skill folders creates fewer temporary path-processing objects during background checks. [#167644](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167644) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway shutdown avoids stopping each native skill watcher individually when the process itself is exiting. [#168273](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168273) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).







#### Native Apps

The Mac app's Retry action can finish repairs to its selected installation and plugins, while mobile updates improve protected connections, chat layout and attachments.




##### Mac Gateway setup and Retry recovery


Retry in the [Mac app](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/mac/bundled-gateway) repairs plugin links against the selected OpenClaw installation, checks the restarted service and clears the incomplete-update warning after required repairs succeed. Recovery also preserves a newer managed Gateway and respects runtime or service choices you change during repair.

These repairs require the updated Mac app. A refused repair remains pending, uncertain cleanup leaves the Gateway stopped, and a Gateway you paused stays paused. If the bundled installer is missing or incompatible, reinstall OpenClaw.app. New service definitions request a longer stop window, but an already loaded job retains its previous deadline; see [restart recovery](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery).



###### Sources and complete change list


**Setup and recovery**

- Retry repairs selected-installation plugin links, verifies service health and clears the matching incomplete-update warning after successful repair [#171508](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171508). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) for the fix and [@shanselman](https://github.com/shanselman) for reporting and investigating it.
- Recovery keeps a newer managed Gateway and uses its CLI to restore a missing Node service [#170766](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170766). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Recovery preserves runtime and service choices changed while a repair is underway [#165044](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165044). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fresh local setup saves a usable Gateway credential and avoids redundant runtime copying and connection waits [#169403](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169403). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Recovery recognizes protected Bun service commands containing `--no-install` [#170006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170006). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jokerman1302-eng](https://github.com/jokerman1302-eng), [@davidmackrory-byte](https://github.com/davidmackrory-byte) and [@josephsilvagnoli](https://github.com/josephsilvagnoli).
- Updates recognize equivalent `/tmp` and `/private/tmp` locations instead of waiting on their own update lock [#169860](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169860). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Service repair can continue after an overlapping load request when checks confirm the Gateway is loaded, leaving running services alone and starting stopped ones [#170740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170740). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- The Mac app waits up to five minutes for an existing Gateway service to finish starting, repeatedly checking that it is still the same process. Fresh installs retain a two-minute wait [#158954](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158954). Thanks [@MertBasar0](https://github.com/MertBasar0) and [@cjn119-ui](https://github.com/cjn119-ui).
- The app preserves its Gateway process's existing startup readiness allowance [3d181e1](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3d181e1f22720fb1f994d7d0cbf9d4446277b174).
- New Mac service definitions request a 330-second stop window; Doctor backs up and migrates recognized older generated definitions while preserving custom settings [#165320](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165320). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@yuzh086-create](https://github.com/yuzh086-create).
- On affected Monterey hosts, native membership checks can unblock service control from an independent terminal. An old installed updater may still need manual service recovery [#163317](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163317). Thanks [@NovaUnboundAi](https://github.com/NovaUnboundAi) for the report and hardware investigation, [@xXG0DLessXx](https://github.com/xXG0DLessXx) for implementation and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) for integration.
- A verified external terminal can restart the Gateway even when an ancestor process cannot be inspected [#170354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170354). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

**Connections and app behavior**

- Status checks for an isolated installation report its selected Gateway as unreachable instead of waiting on another installation's service [#167677](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167677). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Learned secure connections reconnect after a trusted certificate renewal; explicitly configured fingerprints remain fixed [#168151](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168151). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with acknowledgment to [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev) for the earlier work.
- Session actions and sleep/wake retries stay with their original server after switching Gateways, and changes to logging verbosity take effect immediately [#165307](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165307). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Native Claude session discovery honors its catalog opt-out. The Mac microphone panel shares local wake-word capture without sending speech and yields to Talk or push-to-talk [#165318](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165318). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- No-speech feedback gains translations in 21 non-English locales [#165369](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165369).
- Worktree inspection avoids Rosetta native-library crashes through a Git fallback; process inspection requires native arm64 Node [#165088](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165088). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Patch previews and failed-update notifications avoid crashes caused by extreme line coordinates or retry counts [#166036](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166036). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac node-command cleanup releases occupied command slots when process lookup stalls after a process-anchor failure [#166478](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166478). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@hippoley](https://github.com/hippoley).






##### iPhone and iPad


The iOS app can connect and chat through [Cloudflare Access-protected Gateways](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/ios#cloudflare-access-sign-in). Sign in through the browser, tap Done and then Continue, and complete normal Gateway pairing.

Access sign-in does not grant OpenClaw admin permissions. Embedded Dashboard pages and widgets are not supported through this sign-in, and sharing to a protected Gateway completes in the main app.

Compact chat layouts give messages more room, voice replies remain visible in history, and iOS and Mac reconnect sooner after Wi-Fi, cellular or VPN changes. In iPhone and iPad Safari, one attachment action opens Safari's own camera and file choices.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Connections and setup**

- Saved Gateway profiles share Cloudflare Access sign-in while keeping cancellation and local sign-out tied to the right profile [#147238](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147238). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [@Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin).
- Browser sign-in and an explicit Continue handoff enable native chat and authenticated media through Cloudflare Access [#147244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147244). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [@Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin) for the code, [@TheAngryPit](https://github.com/TheAngryPit) for the iPhone connection report and [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant) for review.
- iOS and Mac reconnect sooner after network changes [#168024](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168024). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retiring an iOS operator connection closes its private network transport [b3a37c2](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b3a37c253b7d2e65e7926298a2b933ba8cc658fa).
- Access settings and expired setup-code recovery messages gain translations in 21 non-English locales [#166873](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166873).
- Readiness-check instructions and completion messages gain translations in 21 non-English locales [#167329](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167329).

**Chat and attachments**

- Compact-width chat hides avatars to give replies more space, keeps accessible assistant names and adds sender-side tails to completed standalone bubbles [#166067](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166067). Thanks [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored), [@Peetiegonzalez](https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez) and [@Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin).
- Voice replies retain their model labels and held-answer order, while large continuation and background-task notices fold into expandable labels [#165473](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165473). Thanks [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored), [@Peetiegonzalez](https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez) and [@Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin).
- Long iOS conversations avoid repeated text parsing and menu preparation while a reply streams [#165927](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165927). Thanks [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored), [@Peetiegonzalez](https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez) and [@Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin).
- Chat and New Session use one attachment action in iPhone and iPad Safari, leaving camera and source choices to Safari's native picker [#167590](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167590). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).






##### Android


Android's accent-color choices wrap to fit narrow settings windows, and image attachments release their decoded image memory after conversion. Notification events can also start agent processing when recurring [heartbeats](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/heartbeat) are disabled with `0m`; an empty heartbeat file can still cause that work to be skipped.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Improvements and fixes**

- Accent-color choices wrap without shrinking their controls in narrow settings windows [#165639](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165639). Thanks [@IWhatsskill](https://github.com/IWhatsskill).
- Image attachments release the original decoded bitmap whether JPEG encoding succeeds or fails [#165562](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165562). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Android notification events can trigger processing with periodic heartbeats disabled [#166522](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166522). Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309) and [@BensonChen1204](https://github.com/BensonChen1204).






##### Linux quick chat


Linux Quick Chat respects Escape and your latest show or hide request while the window is still starting, so an early dismissal does not get undone when startup finishes.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Bug fixes**

- Quick Chat preserves the requested visibility through startup [#166673](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166673). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).






##### Windows Gateway service


Eligible [Windows Gateway installations](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/windows) can start before login with a password-free scheduled task, and Doctor can migrate recognized older task definitions. Slow cold starts get longer [readiness windows](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/gateway/restart-and-supervision). This password-free mode cannot use Windows network credentials or encrypted files; fallback modes and node hosts can still require login.

Updating does not always replace the launcher used by existing password-based tasks. A failed service start can also leave readiness checking running for up to 90 minutes.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Startup and repair**

- Eligible Gateway tasks use password-free before-login startup, with longer waits for slow cold starts [#165163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165163). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the changes, [@desksk](https://github.com/desksk) for reporting and investigation and [@runvouch](https://github.com/runvouch) for diagnostic advice.
- Doctor can re-enable and start an owned disabled Gateway task, with accurate disabled-state messages and profile-specific recovery commands [#164728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164728). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@taoxia255-del](https://github.com/taoxia255-del).
- Status preserves the saved Node or Bun selection when Task Scheduler supplies the normal working directory, and repair guidance names Task Scheduler [#165538](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165538). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).






##### Native conversation display


Chat on iPhone, iPad and Mac keeps tool names visible when tools finish running and shows supported saved task progress as labeled bars. Diagrams load again, and inline math leaves later code formatting intact. A reply no longer appears twice when its live text exactly matches the saved message in the same turn.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Chat display**

- Native Tool Search calls show the actual tool name and useful input details, with clearer tool icons across clients [#165095](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165095). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Ended iOS and Mac tool rows retain local tool names when no status is available; iPhone also shows `No result` [#166359](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166359). Thanks [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored), [@Peetiegonzalez](https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez) and [@Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin).
- Apple task lists render supported saved progress markup as labeled bars and omit it from collapsed summaries [#166321](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166321). Thanks [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored), [@Peetiegonzalez](https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez) and [@Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin).
- iOS and Mac hide live reply text that exactly matches recorded assistant text in the current turn, apart from surrounding whitespace [#166313](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166313). Thanks [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored) and [@Peetiegonzalez](https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez).
- Native chat loads bundled Mermaid diagrams from hashed assets [#166680](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166680). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Inline math crossing an earlier code span no longer changes later backtick code into formatted text [#166951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166951). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Background-task and injected-context notice labels gain translations in 21 non-English locales on iOS and Mac [#167408](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167408).






##### Shared native runtime


The shared Bun runtime receives fixes for embedded rendering and worker cleanup, including stopping workers sooner when they reach their memory limit. The obsolete Termux token-sync widget is retired; use [Claude CLI login reuse](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/authentication#anthropic-claude-cli-reuse) for the supported authentication path.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Runtime and authentication**

- Existing shared Bun consumers receive fixes for VM-backed rendering, asynchronous imports and worker heap and stack limits [#165317](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165317). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Shared Bun workers stop sooner at their memory limit and improve memory cleanup while idle on the supported Mac and Linux targets [#166838](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166838). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The obsolete Termux token-sync widget is removed, with guidance to reuse Claude CLI's own login [#165202](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165202). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).






##### Paired computers and device commands


Paired computers keep commands and events working during unrelated device pairing. Commands sent from chats or subagents also keep their [chat and routing information](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/node-exec#channel-and-subagent-context) when both the Gateway and command-line node host are updated. Devices may ask you to approve the added capability again. This change does not update the separate Windows companion or optional Mac app relay.

[Device enrollment](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/session-hosting) offers a copyable command for an optional background session host.

Device requests that were cancelled or lost authorization during a wait stop before more setup runs. Explicit device revocation still blocks subsequent commands. Command failures and connection checks also provide clearer results for people and scripts.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Connections and paired work**

- Commands, requests and events continue through unrelated pairing activity when a pairing change was missed [#169486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169486). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Commands and events remain available while unrelated devices' pairing changes are being saved [#169575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169575). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@gisk0](https://github.com/gisk0).
- Node-executed commands retain chat and subagent routing context when both ends are updated [#164584](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164584). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@idanzhavi09](https://github.com/idanzhavi09), [@MikyWang](https://github.com/MikyWang), [@thearabchad](https://github.com/thearabchad), [@goslingmanagment](https://github.com/goslingmanagment), [@kassol](https://github.com/kassol) and [@vgranciero](https://github.com/vgranciero).
- Paired Macs list Codex sessions with valid current configuration without requiring supervision tools, while respecting the catalog opt-out [#166510](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166510). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Discovering computer tools no longer starts an unused desktop helper; actual use prepares it when needed [#166602](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166602). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Cancelled or no-longer-authorized device requests stop before further readiness checks and provider preparation after a wait [#166608](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166608). Thanks [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).
- Empty desktop and portal streams close after a five-second wait, while streams carrying data can finish draining as progress continues [#165806](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165806). Thanks [@SebTardif](https://github.com/SebTardif) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

**Commands and enrollment**

- Device enrollment supplies a copyable background session-host command and correct instructions for devices already paired [#168623](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168623). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Native-worker sessions finish workspace setup instead of failing before their first model request [#167162](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167162). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Approvals given in time remain usable when command forwarding crosses the original prompt deadline, within the live approval handoff window [#166971](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166971). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Approved POSIX-node commands tolerate trailing whitespace during command-plan matching [#168120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168120). Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
- Commands stopped by a signal on headless nodes report the interruption alongside partial output [#165116](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165116). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Successful node commands stop warning about PATH additions that were never configured [#164506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164506). Thanks [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- `connect --target-file` preserves spaces at filename boundaries so it reads and consumes the intended file [#167116](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167116). Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Refused device commands in JSON mode return structured reasons and recovery guidance while retaining failing exit codes [#167294](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167294). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) for the report and earlier proposal.
- Missing node identity produces a parseable JSON error and recovery guidance without creating an identity [#167437](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167437). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

**Connection checks and resource use**

- Setup and troubleshooting guides use clearer language for existing connection checks [#165736](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165736). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Web and selected native diagnostics use familiar connection-check labels [#165737](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165737). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- CLI, setup and service messages explain existing connection and health checks more clearly [#165740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165740). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Plugin setup and help explain connection and readiness checks in everyday language [#165741](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165741). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Runtime diagnostics use clearer wording for existing checks [#165742](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165742). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Existing native translations use the status wording `Check degraded` [#165959](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165959).

- Cold device-identity preparation moves to a worker, and idle plugin cleanup skips unnecessary database work [#166539](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166539). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).







#### Models and Providers

Model choices now make more use of what your signed-in account offers, with clearer browsing and more consistent conversation settings. This section also covers new provider models, local setup, interrupted replies, and the limits that matter when choosing a route.




##### Browse current models


The browser and terminal model pickers put recommended models and your current choice first, with **All models** and search for the full list. Ordinary browsing reuses the available catalog. Use **Refresh** to look for newly offered models, account details, or models you have pulled locally. See [recommended models](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/recommended-models) and [model commands](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/models).

A listed model still needs the right account access and a compatible runtime. Older OpenAI families disappear from browsing but keep explicit selections on the `openclaw` runtime. Self-hosted Ollama discovery now includes pulled models beyond a nonempty configured list. To keep that list restrictive, use replace mode, disable discovery, or set an agent allowlist.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Improvements**

- Put recommended and current models first in browser and terminal pickers, with All models, search, translated labels, and clearer Claude CLI grouping. Anthropic API choices stay separate. [#167370](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167370), [#167420](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167420), [#167438](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167438). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Reuse published catalogs for ordinary lists and saved-session pickers; explicit Refresh still discovers upstream models and personal account details. [#166037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166037), [#167008](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167008), [#167334](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167334). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep the completed account catalog and prices visible while replacement discovery is pending. Failed discovery can still replace discovered entries with built-in choices. [#167009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167009), [#167178](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167178). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Show known provider models after login while account discovery runs, and restore Claude CLI and Kimi choices when startup discovery completes. Initial rows do not establish account eligibility. [#167458](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167458), [#167460](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167460). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Discover eligible live-listed chat models for OpenAI and xAI API keys. GPT-3.5, GPT-4 including 4o/4.1, and o1/o3/o4 families leave browsing; explicit IDs require the `openclaw` runtime, and some require managed or disabled web search. Unfamiliar models use conservative text-only limits and unknown prices. [#167462](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167462). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus). See [OpenAI model routes](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/models).
- Discover newly pulled self-hosted Ollama chat models on Refresh while preserving configured overrides. A configured list no longer restricts discovery unless replace mode, disabled discovery, or an agent allowlist does so. Hosted `ollama.com` remains configured-only. [#167463](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167463). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus). See [Ollama discovery](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/ollama/model-discovery).
- Admit unfamiliar direct-API Claude models using advertised capabilities, while retaining existing thinking validation. [#167658](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167658). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev), and [NOVA-Openclaw](https://github.com/NOVA-Openclaw).
- Hide provider models missing from a successfully refreshed hosted inventory while preserving exact configured IDs. Those saved IDs do not make a retired model available. [#167855](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167855). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Omit unlisted pro models for ChatGPT-only accounts after successful discovery; usable API keys retain pro choices. [#167975](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167975). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Allow larger upstream model feeds in hosted catalog publishing and add NVIDIA's featured-first recommendation ordering for the next published refresh. [#166699](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166699), [#167989](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167989). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Let model and thinking preferences change while a reply runs. They apply to waiting messages, leaving the running turn on its original settings. [#160440](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160440). Thanks [hxy91819](https://github.com/hxy91819) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Show configured models with missing authentication as unavailable, with Claude login guidance where applicable. A disabled plugin or missing account selection can also need attention. [#166338](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166338). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Explain that a model scan can replace fallback configuration, and document the existing `--no-probe` inspection route. [#161413](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161413). Thanks [ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [Truck0ff](https://github.com/Truck0ff).
- Report rejected or unavailable CLI discovery with readable guidance and machine-readable outcomes. This does not repair xAI account access. [#167128](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167128). Thanks [tianhaotian](https://github.com/tianhaotian), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [ClawMoew](https://github.com/ClawMoew).
- Avoid rebuilding unrelated model information when opening choices, reading personal account labels, or checking background CLI logins. Independent providers can refresh in parallel. [#165708](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165708), [#166434](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166434), [#167101](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167101), [#167359](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167359), [#167447](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167447). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

**Bug fixes**

- Recover first-login model discovery when a reused worker's shared credential store moves. [811d442](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/811d442afa2c1103ed10f33f3b64fb1afa7675f8), [bbdc68b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/bbdc68b94ccf9baa2ac007964bc7db2d1541f8f3).
- Avoid restart warnings for model reload or logout work superseded by newer configuration. Genuine failures still retain restart recovery. [ad332df](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/ad332df677b4897afb22ae6af2fd68f45e35c350), [b755f03](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b755f03cd5a45596e55a4a90a54ec372a3a51074).
- Include discovered models when a usable API key is held through a protected credential reference. [#147374](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147374). Thanks [pheidon](https://github.com/pheidon), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [kybrcore](https://github.com/kybrcore).
- Advance to an eligible configured fallback when Ollama reports a retired model with HTTP 410. [#160047](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160047). Thanks [Irish-Joseph](https://github.com/Irish-Joseph), [benjaml4](https://github.com/benjaml4), and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Retry failed catalog replacement when you next open model choices or send a new message, without replaying existing tasks. Temporary discovery failures also retry with increasing delays. [#162901](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162901), [#167230](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167230). Thanks [scotthuang](https://github.com/scotthuang), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Keep provider-name results separate and let Vertex use refreshed Google authentication tokens without an extra five-minute cache. [#164520](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164520). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep the selected model and picker chevron steady when sending; Send and Stop still show reply progress. [#164963](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164963). Thanks [vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Keep accepted catalogs available if saving a replacement fails, and move database-lock waits away from Gateway callbacks. [#165034](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165034). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Show readable labels for unusual provider IDs, deduplicate Claude CLI choices by configured route, and merge xAI alias groups without invalidating saved selections. API and CLI routes remain distinct. [#165339](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165339), [#166311](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166311), [#167444](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167444). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Return retryable errors after a shared 20-second catalog wait when creating, continuing, or changing sessions. Creation keeps the draft; rejected changes leave their targets unchanged, and bulk changes retain independent successes. Other preparation can take longer. [#165989](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165989), [#166089](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166089), [#166376](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166376). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi), [dprev](https://github.com/dprev), and [ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star).
- Keep another session usable while a model or runtime-consent change prepares in one session. [#166059](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166059). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prefer exact model IDs over colliding aliases and explain unsupported agent-scoped CLI changes. [#166983](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166983). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserve unrelated provider inventory when one provider's configuration changes. [#167117](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167117). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Honor catalog thinking capabilities in commands, replies, and schedules, with explicit configuration taking precedence. Older xAI sign-ins need another login to refresh saved capabilities. [#168566](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168566). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Use the newly saved credential for key discovery and allow OAuth catalog refresh with older or empty agent databases. [#168617](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168617), [#168957](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168957). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Allow guarded model requests to try IPv6 after IPv4 failure when Node address-family autoselection is enabled. [#168973](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168973). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [an2in](https://github.com/an2in), [georgenaz](https://github.com/georgenaz), [motookoyama](https://github.com/motookoyama), and [orthogonalistic](https://github.com/orthogonalistic).






##### Sign-in and account recovery


Sign-in errors in Models → Connect and setup now expose their full details with a copy control. Account selection also survives temporary credential loss when the selection remains compatible. Authentication still needs a valid credential before replies can resume.

[MiniMax sign-ins](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/minimax) now renew tokens for Global and CN accounts. Existing sign-ins, or a change of region, need one new login with `openclaw models auth login --provider minimax-portal`. Claude Code login and logout changes are picked up on demand without a Gateway restart, at most once a minute plus the time needed to check and refresh the catalog.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Improvements**

- Show full copyable sign-in diagnostics in Models → Connect and setup. [#155429](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155429). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Avoid rebuilding unrelated agents' credential state on saves, and move personal OAuth and discovery database work away from Gateway request handling. Accepted personal credential saves finish before shutdown. [#165644](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165644), [#165658](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165658), [#166183](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166183). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Detect Claude Code login or logout on the next eligible demand check, without restarting the Gateway or reloading the page. [#166305](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166305). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

**Bug fixes**

- Renew MiniMax Global and CN OAuth tokens using the correct regional service. Rerun `openclaw models auth login --provider minimax-portal` for older sign-ins or region changes; custom proxies do not redirect refresh tokens. [#160459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160459). Thanks [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino), [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [rongtianhua](https://github.com/rongtianhua), and [mychataa](https://github.com/mychataa).
- Preserve newer settings while GitHub authentication recovery reads configuration asynchronously. [#166936](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166936). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with acknowledgment to [Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen) for prior work.
- Retain an explicitly selected compatible account through temporary credential loss, while continuing to show that it cannot authenticate. [#166985](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166985). Thanks [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Recover native interactive ChatGPT conversations after an expired connection is reconnected, using saved history within existing retry limits. HTTP chat-completions streams can still fail on this recovery path. [#167063](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167063). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [jackten](https://github.com/jackten).
- Show failed GitHub account discovery as a compact dismissible retry card, while keeping actual publication results and uncertain attempts visible. [#167375](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167375). Thanks [jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Stop missing optional quota or refresh fields from aborting replies. This does not change account quotas or resolve every OAuth storage error. [#170065](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170065). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [modlethub](https://github.com/modlethub), and [Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx).






##### ChatGPT and Codex conversations


Gateway chats using the default Codex runtime can use models offered by your ChatGPT account even when OpenClaw's built-in route list does not yet recognize them. If an unfamiliar model offers both API-key and subscription routes, the subscription can now be preferred. Direct CLI runs and utility completions retain their existing limits. See [OpenAI runtimes](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/runtimes).

ChatGPT discovery uses the Codex version bundled with OpenClaw, so models requiring a newer client wait for a bundle update. Interrupted conversations also gain bounded recovery for overloads and unfinished connections. If Codex rejects a thread for exceeding its history limit, start a new session and resend the message.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Improvements**

- Honor supported maximum thinking in Codex scheduled and webhook runs. [#165444](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165444). Thanks [Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).
- Keep ChatGPT discovery aligned with bundled Codex compatibility. The intermediate npm-latest discovery policy is reverted, so newer version-gated models wait for a bundled update. [#167387](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167387), [#167633](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167633). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Read compressed conversation history with less repeated copying and allocation. [#166778](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166778). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Explain Codex history-limit rejection with instructions to start a new session and resend, without automatically continuing the rejected turn. [#167016](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167016). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [jaikharbanda](https://github.com/jaikharbanda).

**Bug fixes**

- Exclude an incompatible bundled visualization skill when Codex discovers it, while keeping OpenClaw widgets available. Discovery failure leaves normal turns usable and allows another check later. [#165728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165728). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Make Codex selectable for eligible official OpenAI Responses API-key models after startup and after deferred database recovery. Existing endpoint and explicit-runtime restrictions still apply. [#165979](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165979), [#166081](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166081). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retry one harmless concurrent state-read change during Codex preparation, while rejecting genuine ownership or authority conflicts. [#166010](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166010). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Carry the current request and completed-work context into bounded overload recovery. [#166868](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166868). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [Baumus](https://github.com/Baumus).
- Enter existing recovery when a ChatGPT connection closes normally before the response finishes. Completed responses and policy-related closes retain their handling. [#167512](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167512). Thanks [ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [mightyx](https://github.com/mightyx), and [aleps001](https://github.com/aleps001).
- Run account-listed ChatGPT models through the default Codex runtime in foreground Gateway chats, including unfamiliar model IDs. Existing credential policy still governs access. [#168033](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168033). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Retry one rejected cached WebSocket continuation before response events begin, without repeating completed tools. Other continuation failures can still occur. [#168055](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168055). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [nikosml](https://github.com/nikosml).
- Restore a missing answer ending when the completed Codex message matches the text already streamed. Divergent completed text does not replace the stream. [#168400](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168400). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Retain supported Ultrafast mode on resumed conversations and follow-ups, subject to account and model eligibility. [#169265](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169265). Thanks [SanderHelgesen](https://github.com/SanderHelgesen).
- Allow compatible follow-ups after thread preparation fails while background commands continue. Incompatible policy changes remain refused. [#169431](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169431). Thanks [jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).






##### Claude CLI conversations


Claude CLI conversations keep the latest history across web and channel replies, apply changed instructions when resuming, and honor per-session MCP tool toggles on warm turns. These changes prevent new history forks from the affected path; they do not merge existing branches or repair manual compaction.

Earlier Claude CLI sign-ins can gain provider-wide routing through `openclaw update` or `openclaw doctor --fix`. The repair targets eligible CLI-default configurations and preserves explicit API routes. If an API credential exists only in the auth store, the repair cannot see it and can route unpinned models to Claude Code. Keep an explicit API route for models you want to use that way. See [Claude CLI setup](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cli-backends) and [Doctor migrations](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations).



###### Sources and complete change list


**Improvements**

- Guide Claude CLI toward registered tool names and discovery without adding permissions or registering missing tools. [#153032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153032). Thanks [kiranvk-2011](https://github.com/kiranvk-2011), [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [lakemike](https://github.com/lakemike), [philknows](https://github.com/philknows), [destinedenergy](https://github.com/destinedenergy), and [wbumgardner-max](https://github.com/wbumgardner-max).
- Document the existing primary Claude CLI runtime, fallback behavior, and canonical setup examples. [#166319](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166319). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Allow history paging before a Claude CLI conversation's reset boundary. [#166578](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166578). Thanks [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).

**Bug fixes**

- Stop repeated streaming wrappers from exhausting Claude CLI's useful-output budget. The output limit still applies. [#153545](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153545). Thanks [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt), [methylester](https://github.com/methylester), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc), [aaajiao](https://github.com/aaajiao), [telbus](https://github.com/telbus), [aoclaw-glitch](https://github.com/aoclaw-glitch), [aikohoshinoai-prog](https://github.com/aikohoshinoai-prog), and [RTapiador](https://github.com/RTapiador).
- Avoid saving standalone interrupted tool markup as conversation history. Previously malformed history is unchanged. [#165031](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165031). Thanks [jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [ndakota79](https://github.com/ndakota79).

- Resume the same latest history across web and channel turns while keeping route credentials and private approvals separate. Existing branches and manual-compaction issues still need separate recovery. [#167241](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167241). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [cmatrx0](https://github.com/cmatrx0).

[#167817](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167817). Thanks [yu-xin-c](https://github.com/yu-xin-c), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [robburke](https://github.com/robburke).
