- Route newly signed-in Claude CLI accounts provider-wide, and repair eligible earlier CLI-default configurations through `openclaw update` or `openclaw doctor --fix`. Exact model routes and authored API choices remain authoritative; auth-store-only credentials are outside the repair's checks. Fresh sign-ins stop automatically adding deprecated models, while existing entries remain usable. [#167963](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167963), [#168074](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168074). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Honor per-session MCP tool toggles on warm Claude CLI turns. [#168393](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168393). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [dprev](https://github.com/dprev).
- Preserve the originating tool allowance for background continuations, while enforcing later restrictions and read-only downgrades. [#168670](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168670). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).






##### OpenAI replies and speed controls


OpenAI speed controls reflect the model and account's supported modes. Daybreak Blue offers Standard and Fast; Daybreak Red offers Standard. Neither offers Ultrafast. A supported Ultrafast choice stays available when a request receives slower fulfillment, with a temporary explanation in the picker. The provider still determines the tier actually delivered. See [Fast mode](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/advanced#fast-mode).

Replies also handle larger ChatGPT HTTP streams and more tool schemas. Built-in OpenAI fallback choices can run without enabling the Codex plugin, while an explicit Codex runtime selection still requires it.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Improvements**

- Apply Daybreak Blue and Red speed eligibility, with safe tier-rejection recovery before output or tools begin. [#164597](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164597). Thanks [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Release completed Responses conversation memory while preserving the existing 90-minute follow-up reuse window. [#165142](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165142). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Add task and style guidance for supported GPT-5 and GPT-6 models, including GPT-6 Astra. [#166152](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166152). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep supported Ultrafast available after slower fulfillment, showing a temporary tooltip instead of removing the option. [#165990](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165990). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Use Responses for managed official OpenAI Completions requests combining reasoning and tools. Proxies, Azure, and direct SDK calls keep their routes. [#166182](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166182). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [astutoai](https://github.com/astutoai).
- Allow ChatGPT HTTP streams to exceed 16 MiB in total while retaining the 16 MiB limit on an individual buffered event. [#166853](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166853). Thanks [Jackten](https://github.com/Jackten) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Accept affected tool schemas containing regular-expression lookarounds through the schema fallback. [#166999](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166999). Thanks [scs0209](https://github.com/scs0209), [adamjgmiller](https://github.com/adamjgmiller), and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Provide a runnable built-in OpenAI fallback when the Codex plugin is disabled, preserving explicit runtime selections. [#169412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169412). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).






##### Gemini, Vertex, and Gemma


[Vertex setup guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/google) now explains Application Default Credentials, project and location settings, and metadata-service authentication. Truncated Gemini and Vertex streams can enter existing retry or fallback handling. Gemini responses that reach a generation limit keep their partial text without executing incomplete tool calls.

Managed Gemma 4 conversations can recover complete tool batches returned as final text. Examples, malformed calls, and direct-mode replies do not authorize tool execution, and existing permissions still apply.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Improvements**

- Document Vertex Application Default Credentials, project/location setup, and metadata authentication without treating an OAuth token as an API key. [#150593](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150593). Thanks [jayswu](https://github.com/jayswu), [jayden](https://github.com/jayden), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [gadgrandez](https://github.com/gadgrandez).

**Bug fixes**

- Treat cut Gemini and Vertex stream events as eligible for existing retry or failover, while leaving complete malformed events outside this new recovery. [#165870](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165870). Thanks [ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin) and [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino).
- Recover complete final-text tool batches for managed Gemma 4 OpenAI-compatible conversations. Direct mode, prose examples, malformed calls, and unsafe endings do not execute recovered tools. [#167886](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167886). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna).
- Preserve partial Gemini content at generation limits and keep incomplete function calls non-executable. The same update includes a computer-use driver with upstream fixes for macOS app enumeration and cursor behavior, and Windows installation. [#168103](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168103). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).






##### Local model setup and reasoning


Local setup can offer to download a selected llama.cpp model with its size and your consent, reinstall a missing current managed program, and recover an exactly identified leftover managed service on supported Apple silicon Macs. Managed macOS binaries require macOS 13.3 or later; custom and remote alternatives remain available. See [llama.cpp setup](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/llama-cpp) and [local model services](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/local-model-services).

LM Studio gains refreshed model discovery and a two-minute default load budget. Rerun setup to repair older graded thinking mappings. For older generated llama.cpp capability overrides, remove the affected model row, load the model, then Refresh; rerunning setup alone preserves those overrides. Ollama waits for completion before sending final reply chunks, keeping interim tool narration out of those messages. Long tool-free answers can wait too.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Improvements**

- Map vLLM Qwen thinking efforts to its chat-template controls and send the expected DeepSeek V4 controls. [#150149](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150149). Thanks [leapdragon](https://github.com/leapdragon), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [krell11](https://github.com/krell11). See [vLLM](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/vllm).
- Check the managed llama.cpp macOS 13.3 requirement before downloads when the OS version is readable, with guidance toward custom or remote alternatives. [#163358](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163358). Thanks [NovaUnboundAi](https://github.com/NovaUnboundAi), [xXG0DLessXx](https://github.com/xXG0DLessXx), and [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Explain a smaller local embedding-only setup for low-memory systems, with explicit selection and download consent. The roughly 0.3 GB model download is not a total RAM requirement. [#165832](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165832). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [NovaUnboundAi](https://github.com/NovaUnboundAi).
- Give llama.cpp Metal executable validation up to two minutes rather than 15 seconds, while retaining cancellation. Genuinely hung checks can take longer to fail. [#167630](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167630). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Offer a selected, undownloaded llama.cpp model during setup with size information and consent. Declining preserves configured routes. [#168064](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168064). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Refresh LM Studio's available models while preserving saved settings and explicit replace mode. [#168039](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168039). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [jb-lopez](https://github.com/jb-lopez). See [LM Studio](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/lmstudio).

**Bug fixes**

- Restore the exact current managed llama.cpp executable if it is missing. Custom, relocated, older-release, and foreign-platform paths remain outside automatic repair. [#153523](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153523). Thanks [samuelreyes982](https://github.com/samuelreyes982), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [dh-js](https://github.com/dh-js).
- Hold native Ollama tool narration out of durable replies until completion. Long tool-free answers also wait; supported live previews can still update, and a single-message result is not guaranteed. [#156389](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156389). Thanks [Olli0103](https://github.com/Olli0103), [Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [noamliav](https://github.com/noamliav), and [solutionsbyvance](https://github.com/solutionsbyvance).
- Pass a plugin's explicit thinking-off choice to native Ollama when configuration leaves it unset. Explicit configuration remains authoritative. [#159380](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159380). Thanks [YangManBOBO](https://github.com/YangManBOBO), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [hsheresd](https://github.com/hsheresd), and [sxh313](https://github.com/sxh313).
- Map LM Studio's max thinking to the highest supported canonical effort. Rerun setup to replace older saved graded mappings. [#164478](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164478). Thanks [ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Recover exactly identified orphaned managed llama.cpp services on supported Apple silicon macOS hosts. Update the host and plugin together; custom or ambiguous processes remain untouched. [#167902](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167902). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [TomSchuman](https://github.com/TomSchuman).
- Handle tool-name alias wrappers on native Ollama and identified Ollama-compatible chat routes, keeping stored tool names canonical. This does not resolve the broader reported Windows reply issue. [#167933](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167933). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Allow two minutes by default for LM Studio model loading and stop failed capacity upgrades with guidance. Existing timeout overrides remain available. [#168044](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168044). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Honor the selected llama.cpp model alias and surface credential-save failures during setup. [#168232](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168232). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

For older generated overrides, remove the affected model row, load the model, then Refresh; setup alone preserves overrides. [#168309](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168309). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [guarismo](https://github.com/guarismo).






##### Meta and other providers


[Meta](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/meta) adds `meta/muse-wonder-1.0`, the Wonder Standard API model, with image input, tools, streaming, and a 1,048,576-token context window. Wonder and Spark 1.3 Standard gain max thinking. High remains the default, off maps to minimal, and Spark stays the onboarding choice. Wonder Full, Contributor, and Muse Code are separate offerings.

Other changes cover Copilot model routes, Haiku 5.5, MiniMax M3.1 Flash Preview, and provider tools. Existing [xAI OAuth](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/xai) users need `openclaw models auth login --provider xai --method oauth` to refresh Grok thinking choices. For saved [Agents API sessions](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/agentsapi), keep the original key, model, environment, and MCP settings until their first continuation converts them. A downgrade needs the matching backup from before the upgrade.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Improvements**

- Add Muse Wonder Standard with image input, tools, streaming, and max thinking; Spark 1.3 Standard also gains max. Catalog prices are $4 input, $0.20 cached input, and $20 output per million tokens. OpenClaw's 131,072-token output cap is not a vendor-published maximum. Spark remains the onboarding default; Wonder Full, Contributor, and Muse Code are not added. [#170232](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170232). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Add eligible MiniMax M3.1 Flash Preview choices with five thinking efforts. Max is its default and off maps to max; M3 remains the provider default. Bundled zero prices mean unknown rates, not free use. [#160089](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160089). Thanks [NianJiuZst](https://github.com/NianJiuZst), [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [Takhoffman](https://github.com/Takhoffman), [php737](https://github.com/php737), [ns-spec](https://github.com/ns-spec), [Hansen1018](https://github.com/Hansen1018), and [olveww-dot](https://github.com/olveww-dot).
- Add the Haiku 5.5 alias and use it as the utility default, with optional adaptive thinking and long-context pricing. Explicit Haiku 4.5 selections remain available. [#166706](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166706). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa). See [Anthropic models](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/anthropic).
- Use supported routes for Copilot's live-listed models and recognize eligible Claude adaptive-thinking metadata. [#167941](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167941). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Use Grok's advertised thinking efforts. Existing xAI OAuth users must rerun `openclaw models auth login --provider xai --method oauth`; models that cannot disable reasoning do not offer off. [#167949](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167949). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Cache ClawRouter inventory for up to an hour, with Refresh available for a current list. Current grants and budgets still govern requests. [#168050](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168050). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

**Bug fixes**

- Resolve the existing Together `togetherai`, StepFun `stepfun-ai`/`stepfun-ai-step-plan`, and Kilo `kilo/` aliases. [#144946](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144946), [#144951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144951), [#144960](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144960). Thanks [ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Size Copilot execution using the accepted account model capacity instead of a synthetic 128K estimate. Refresh older discovery caches; context reporting has separate handling. [#156636](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156636). Thanks [saju01](https://github.com/saju01), [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [snotty](https://github.com/snotty), [vinaycodeaxe](https://github.com/vinaycodeaxe), [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt), and [Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx).
- Preserve the selected model for OpenCode Go image requests, avoiding the affected MissingSessionID failure. [#160604](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160604). Thanks [sxh313](https://github.com/sxh313), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [Lian-Hu-Agent](https://github.com/Lian-Hu-Agent).
- Honor the selected region and transport for custom Bedrock providers. [#164406](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164406). Thanks [Ayushdevo](https://github.com/Ayushdevo), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [kai-ocean-kurt](https://github.com/kai-ocean-kurt).
- Keep Copilot plugin and MCP tools available after the first turn. [#164598](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164598). Thanks [ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin) and [takyyon](https://github.com/takyyon).
- Rename Kimi Code Mode's wait argument to `awaitResults`. Update applicable callers; the former key is ignored rather than treated as an alias. [#165022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165022). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). See [Code Mode's tool surface](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/code-mode/tool-surface).
- Report missing output promptly when local Comfy finishes without media, while retaining sanitized upstream execution errors. [#166249](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166249). Thanks [chelsealong](https://github.com/chelsealong), [catnipluss](https://github.com/catnipluss), and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Apply valid model-specific thinking defaults on OpenRouter's first turn. Mandatory reasoning and off support still depend on the model. [#166705](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166705). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Replace a stopped Crabbox even when its tool-call ID is reused; duplicate live calls still reuse the current box. [#167058](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167058). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
- Preserve native tools, search policy, filesystem settings, and cleanup on Agents API follow-ups. Changed search policy requires `/new` or `/reset`; deleting a local session leaves remote history and files intact. [#168067](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168067). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Convert supported saved Agents API sessions on their first continuation. Keep the original key, model, environment, and MCP settings until conversion; downgrade requires the matching backup from before the upgrade. [#168150](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168150). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Send runtime context in the supported user role for affected compatible and local Responses routes. Native provider roles continue to follow their capabilities. [#168546](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168546). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [flanker-stack](https://github.com/flanker-stack).






##### Thinking and context budgets


Thinking choices now reach custom OpenAI-compatible models marked as supporting reasoning. This changes existing configurations too. If your endpoint rejects the effort field, set `compat.supportsReasoningEffort: false` on that model. On custom routes, off usually omits the field and leaves the server's default in control; it does not guarantee zero reasoning. See [custom endpoint thinking settings](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/thinking#custom-openai-compatible-endpoints).

Context refill leaves room for system instructions and compaction, though a large new tool result can still exceed the model's window. Compatible providers can also return completed nonstreaming results when configured with `params.streaming: false`.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Improvements**

- Support completed nonstreaming text, usage, tools, and reasoning for compatible models configured with `params.streaming: false`. Streaming remains the default; native Ollama is unchanged. [#168057](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168057). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep the previous usable thinking choices when a catalog refresh fails. Initial loads without a snapshot and unavailable models still need their underlying issue resolved. [#146155](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146155). Thanks [ngo-a](https://github.com/ngo-a) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Hide recognized unfinished reasoning blocks after token limits, errors, or cancellation. This requires a recognized opening marker and is not a universal privacy filter. [#156939](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156939). Thanks [quinszouls](https://github.com/quinszouls), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [ingenuity2k](https://github.com/ingenuity2k).
- Reserve system and compaction space when refilling conversation history during tool work. New oversized tool output can still overflow the window. [#166120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166120). Thanks [ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [igs-rogenlo](https://github.com/igs-rogenlo).
- Honor caller budgets for compatible embedding requests, including managed llama.cpp. Deep status queries default to 60 seconds remotely and 300 seconds locally, with provider overrides. [#167891](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167891). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [holny](https://github.com/holny), [fonoempresa](https://github.com/fonoempresa), and [KarbonXX](https://github.com/KarbonXX).
- Send enabled thinking levels by default for compatible custom reasoning models. Endpoints rejecting the field need `compat.supportsReasoningEffort: false`; off omission leaves the server default, and advanced efforts still require declared support. [#168056](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168056). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [j-nolan](https://github.com/j-nolan).






##### Tool calls and completed answers


Completed Astra answers survive the affected silent background follow-ups within the same attempt, and eligible parallel tool results reach Astra when it yields. Later substantive answers can replace earlier ones. [Tool Search](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/tool-search) also passes deferred images as images to models that can read them, while argument handling preserves valid identifiers and gives clearer input errors.

For endpoints using [DeepSeek response formatting](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/deepseek), complete recognized tool blocks can become tool calls in managed conversations. Malformed or unfinished blocks are hidden without execution. Direct replies and tool-free finalization hide the markup too, but never recover tool actions. Existing tool permissions remain in force.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Improvements**

- Reuse prepared Tool Search text while checking current tool permissions before use. [#165491](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165491). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Guide Codex to call already listed tools directly, while retaining discovery when needed. [#165508](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165508). Thanks [mbelinky](https://github.com/mbelinky).
- Support opt-in Codex hooks across hosts with an HTTPS callback and private credential directory. These hooks trust the operating-system user running the harness. [#166400](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166400). Thanks [Kimiyu-186](https://github.com/Kimiyu-186). See [runtime hooks](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/codex-harness-runtime/hooks).
- Parse proven-safe additions to long replies incrementally, retaining full parsing for uncertain directives, code, or media. [#166782](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166782). Thanks [boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Include core argument hints in large Code Mode tool catalogs and explain where declaration content is returned. Tool APIs and permissions are unchanged. [#166785](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166785), [#167286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167286). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [rclarke0](https://github.com/rclarke0).

**Bug fixes**

- Stop repeated usage or billing events from counting as model progress. Keepalive comments in affected managed compatible streams keep connections alive while preserving the separate progress deadline. Azure Responses and direct Completions are unchanged. [#145850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145850), [#152705](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152705). Thanks [RileyJJY](https://github.com/RileyJJY), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [elharonyclaw](https://github.com/elharonyclaw), [anyech](https://github.com/anyech), [ZengWen-DT](https://github.com/ZengWen-DT), and [alfredjbclaw](https://github.com/alfredjbclaw).
- Preserve valid string identifiers when repairing tool arguments that allow multiple input types. [#158974](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158974). Thanks [Shenrui-Ma](https://github.com/Shenrui-Ma) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Allow one guarded continuation after an eligible malformed later tool call, carrying completed work forward. Existing safety checks and the shared recovery limit remain. [#164274](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164274). Thanks [fransqaas](https://github.com/fransqaas) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Handle large Unicode Tool Search descriptions without the tokenizer overflow. [#164737](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164737). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep valid annotated OpenAI tool schemas strict and ignore invalid nonfinite OpenRouter cache durations. Trajectory exports now use SQLite events only; older sidecar files are neither migrated nor deleted and no longer supply export events. Preserve historical diagnostics before removing those files manually. [#164738](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164738). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). See [trajectory exports](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/trajectory).
- Preserve completed Astra answers through affected silent follow-ups and conditional unfinished-work checks within one attempt. Later substantive, media, or voice answers supersede earlier output; retries and compaction reset this retention. [#164774](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164774), [#164896](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164896). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Report rejected nested Code Mode arguments as input errors rather than internal errors, without automatically rerunning the tool. [#165008](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165008). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prevent Code Mode guests from replacing real tool replies with forged failures. External experimental executors must adopt pull-based settlement as described in the [executor guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/code-mode/executors). [#165071](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165071). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Distinguish tool-call identities across responses and turns for Code Mode, computer/mobile tools, GitHub publication, finalization, and client calls. Same-message replay retains its identity; existing transcripts are not rewritten. [#165210](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165210), [#165212](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165212), [#165297](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165297). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserve prose paragraphs containing pipe operators during reply cleanup. [#165753](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165753). Thanks [Sedrak-Hovhannisyan](https://github.com/Sedrak-Hovhannisyan) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Deliver deferred Tool Search images as images to image-capable models, under existing permissions. [#165762](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165762). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna).
- Preserve native Codex history and search policy for forks and catalog continuations, using temporary threads for temporary tool restrictions. [#166787](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166787). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Include eligible parallel direct-function results when Astra yields. `async: false` opts out; this behavior does not extend to Code Mode, Tool Search catalog calls, or other providers. [#166876](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166876). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Count completed asynchronous tools as progress so a later output cap does not cause a false stalled-run cancellation. Failed or blocked tools do not count. [#166997](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166997). Thanks [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph), and [hippoley](https://github.com/hippoley).
- Refuse Codex input-hook access to unrelated conversation trees and restrict guidance on limited turns. Optional hooks do not provide operating-system isolation. [#167075](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167075). Thanks [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Recover valid recognized DeepSeek-format tool blocks in managed turns and hide internal markup in direct replies and finalization. Malformed or unfinished blocks stay non-executable, and normal permissions still apply. [#169728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169728). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [TOMUIV](https://github.com/TOMUIV).






##### Provider connections and proxy limits


Stalled TCP or TLS reconnects use the normal 10-second connection deadline unless you configure another provider timeout. Requests waiting for response headers also have bounds, defaulting to five minutes locally and two minutes for cloud providers, within the whole-run limit. Cold model loading before headers counts toward that budget; [custom providers](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/model-providers/custom-providers) may need a longer timeout.

Copilot and Mantle catalog requests have a known proxy limitation. They may bypass ordinary `HTTPS_PROXY` routing when `OPENCLAW_PROXY_ACTIVE` is unset, which can prevent discovery or starter-model selection on proxy-only networks.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Bug fixes**

- Bound stalled TCP/TLS reconnects with the normal 10-second default connection deadline, retaining explicit provider timeout budgets. [#167013](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167013). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [sszyh2026](https://github.com/sszyh2026), and [Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx).
- Bound requests stuck before response headers with 300-second local and 120-second cloud defaults, within the whole-run deadline. Cold loads count before headers and may need a longer provider timeout. [#168282](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168282). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx).

**Known limitation**

- Copilot and Mantle catalog transport may bypass ordinary `HTTPS_PROXY` when `OPENCLAW_PROXY_ACTIVE` is unset, breaking discovery or starter selection on proxy-only networks. [#165371](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165371). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).






##### Token, cache, and usage reporting


Context reports distinguish a known capacity from an unknown one, and missing provider usage can use approximate active-context estimates. Enabled diagnostics also count more quiet replies and successful utility work. These estimates do not recover historical usage or establish agreement with a provider invoice. See [context reporting](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/context) and [Prometheus metrics](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/prometheus).

Supported direct OpenAI Responses and Anthropic routes can retain prompt-cache reuse through instruction refreshes. Optional TTL pruning expands to OpenRouter Grok and Gemini 2.5/3, and stays off by default for them. Cache diagnostics explain observed differences; they do not change requests or promise savings. See [prompt caching](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/prompt-caching).



###### Sources and complete change list


**Improvements**

- Record usage and estimated cost for quiet replies and silent heartbeat work when diagnostics are enabled and billable usage is reported. [#152396](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152396). Thanks [ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559), [MutenCath](https://github.com/MutenCath), and [wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666).
- Page context and attachment scans to bound individual memory spikes. Full-result consumers still retain the complete result, and incomplete scans do not justify deleting attachments. [#165221](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165221). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Estimate active context and compaction needs when provider usage is missing, while preserving measured zero values. Billing and older missing records are not reconstructed. [#166151](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166151). Thanks [fede-kamel](https://github.com/fede-kamel), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [DigitalCharon](https://github.com/DigitalCharon).
- Expose current work and session activity in Prometheus. Counts overlap and must not be added together; unavailable samples differ from zero, and zero is not proof that suspension is safe. The same change prevents valid [public conversation links](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/urls) from appearing missing while sharing details refresh; temporary-unavailable reads can be retried, and revocation remains enforced. [#166387](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166387). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserve prompt-cache reuse across instruction refreshes on supported direct native OpenAI Responses and Anthropic routes. Proxies, ChatGPT, Azure Responses, and OpenAI Completions are excluded; resets, compaction, and route or profile changes start a new series. [#167698](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167698). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Allow opt-in TTL pruning for OpenRouter Grok and Gemini 2.5/3 under existing pruning rules. It stays off by default for these providers; older Gemini, Gemma, and OpenRouter OpenAI remain excluded. [#169548](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169548). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [gregb100](https://github.com/gregb100).

**Bug fixes**

- Use the last trusted matching model capacity when current metadata is unavailable, and show `?` or JSON `null` when current model/runtime capacity is unknown. These are reporting changes, not larger execution windows. [#159731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159731), [#165991](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165991). Thanks [Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [nhhc-lxr](https://github.com/nhhc-lxr), [vinaycodeaxe](https://github.com/vinaycodeaxe), and [snotty](https://github.com/snotty).
- Keep Bedrock tool ordering stable to avoid that source of prompt-cache invalidation. [#161838](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161838). Thanks [ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [netbr3ak](https://github.com/netbr3ak).
- Reject calendar dates skipped in the Gateway's timezone. Explicit timezone and UTC controls retain their behavior. [#165523](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165523). Thanks [SashaMIT](https://github.com/SashaMIT) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Compare content-free cache diagnostics with the correct session during concurrent work, identifying the first changed request segment without changing provider requests or billing. [#167890](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167890). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Count successful title and activity utility usage, including final CLI totals, in enabled diagnostics and estimated costs. No historical backfill or invoice parity is implied. [#167986](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167986). Thanks [ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), with acknowledgment to [ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225) for prior work, [etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm) for reporting, and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for verification.







#### Automations and Scheduling

Automations make model choices clearer, let scheduled turns wait for a busy conversation, and give eligible interrupted runs a path to continue.




##### Automation controls and reports


New automation model selections keep the provider you chose, including when two providers use the same model name. Automation prompts also appear as expandable chat rows, so you can read the conversation without losing access to the full instructions or the originating run.

[Team Reports](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/team-reports) waits through a five-minute startup grace period, then catches up at the next eligible scheduled collection. Depending on your schedule, that can be hours after restarting; manual generation remains available immediately. Catch-up also keeps the missed day before startup when the next collection crosses UTC midnight.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Controls and display**

- Refresh suggested models when the selected agent changes without discarding open drafts or the All agents task scope. [#164357](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164357) Thanks to [TUARAN](https://github.com/TUARAN) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Keep same-named models from different providers distinct when saving a selection. Existing saved choices without a provider are not corrected automatically. [#164574](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164574) Thanks to [ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Allow editing of secondary-agent heartbeat prompts migrated by Doctor. [#165064](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165064) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuse unchanged automation lists and scheduler status to reduce repeated server work while preserving task visibility and delivery previews. [#165629](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165629) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Make automation toggles in the pull request checks popup respond immediately and save independently, with errors still visible. Try again checks the saved state without repeating an uncertain change. [#166976](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166976) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Collapse automation prompts into separate expandable chat rows when their originating run is recorded. [#167600](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167600) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

**Commands and reports**

- Preserve escaped spaces in exit-triggered commands and retain edits made while an older run finishes. Reapply the intended `--on-exit` command if it was already trimmed. [#161321](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161321) Thanks to [LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Keep Codex automation app-access request timeouts consistent when the computer's calendar clock changes. [#155855](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155855) Thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
- Defer automatic Team Reports catch-up until an eligible collection after the startup grace period. [#165356](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165356) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retain the missed day before startup when Team Reports catch-up crosses UTC midnight. This does not backfill every day of a longer outage. [#165780](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165780) Thanks to [yetval](https://github.com/yetval).






##### Scheduled runs and saved state


Scheduled turns aimed at a busy conversation wait for its current work to finish before preparing their context. A full reminder queue now reports overflow instead of silently discarding an older reminder. Retry rejected reminders after the queue drains; pending reminders still do not survive a restart.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Conversation context and results**

- Wait for active conversation work and its final saves before preparing a scheduled turn, avoiding interference with an in-progress history summary. [#165310](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165310) Thanks to [Linux2010](https://github.com/Linux2010), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [automaton1379](https://github.com/automaton1379), and [Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).
- Keep existing subagents' memory access when a scheduled job reuses their chat. An actual chat reset still revokes that access. [#166417](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166417) Thanks to [jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Retain a waiting scheduled run's captured conversation context when a later incoming message is saved. [#166487](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166487) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [AXEG0](https://github.com/AXEG0), and [hippoley](https://github.com/hippoley).
- Save the destination conversation after a scheduled command announcement without a source chat, avoiding a misleading session-policy warning after successful delivery. [#166836](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166836) Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [jlahullier](https://github.com/jlahullier).
- Restore the scheduled job ID in plugin completion hooks. [#167124](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167124) Thanks to [xyrolle](https://github.com/xyrolle) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Preserve a removed active job's cancelled status in saved conversation history after cleanup. [#167524](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167524) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Stop fresh scheduled-job sessions inheriting misleading prompt-cache drop warnings from an earlier session. [#167608](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167608) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

**Scheduling and maintenance**

- Preserve accepted reminders when the queue fills, reporting overflow as a failed scheduled run or HTTP 503 for webhooks. [#165305](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165305) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Coordinate scheduled-job and approval saves, and recheck standing approvals for expiry after waiting for pending changes. [#164805](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164805) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Move scheduled-run conversation preparation and saves to database workers, reducing database work on the main server thread. [#165332](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165332) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Wait for agent database preparation before startup automation cleanup, avoiding a premature maintenance failure. [#165814](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165814) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Remove Claw-created agents through the CLI while scheduling is running, preserving unrelated jobs. If the reply is lost, inspect `claws status` before retrying. [#166889](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166889) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserve the historical agent owner of ownerless scheduled jobs when Doctor upgrades legacy agent lists. [#166996](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166996) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduce the conversation data scanned during scheduled-session cleanup while retaining running-job safeguards. [#167248](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167248) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoid premature interruption of Doctor, updates, and outgoing-message work when clock differences produce unusually long expiry waits. [#167467](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167467) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Release database write transactions when scheduled work waits too long for the host to accept it, then retry the work. [#168291](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168291) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna).

**Guidance**

- Explain why a trailing `NO_REPLY` suppresses a text announcement, even after substantive report text. See [silent-token suppression](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/cron#silent-token-suppression). [#161964](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161964) Thanks to [heranliu](https://github.com/heranliu), [jackson2w](https://github.com/jackson2w), and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Clarify [condition testing](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/schedules) with `--due`. The CLI forces a run by default; the agent tool checks whether it is due by default. [#165828](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165828) Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [lidbai](https://github.com/lidbai).






##### Automation results and recovery


New isolated automations created in Control UI or WebChat can [return results to the conversation that created them](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/delivery) when no external delivery route exists. That destination is fixed at creation, and resetting or deleting the conversation prevents the return. Existing jobs are not updated automatically.

Accepted manual runs keep their permitted tools after the starting chat turn ends. Revoking the initiating device after acceptance no longer stops the run; disable or remove the job to cancel it.

For temporary provider failures, automations use their existing bounded retry window before sending failure alerts. Eligible dashboard work interrupted by a restart can also resume after startup, while explicit stops remain cancelled.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Results and recovery**

- Return isolated automation results to their captured creating conversation without giving the run that conversation's history. Jobs created through the CLI without conversation context still need a delivery route. [#167363](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167363) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Allow existing bounded provider-outage retries before requesting owner repair or sending failure alerts. Persistent failures resume normal notification after the retry budget. [#167957](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167957) Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Keep accepted manual automations' permitted tools available after the starting chat turn ends. Disabling or removing the job still cancels it. [#168037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168037) Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Let a configured backup model answer when a primary-model timeout previously caused a saved-conversation error, preserving one saved prompt and final answer. [#168605](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168605) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [wongwilliam425-ui](https://github.com/wongwilliam425-ui).
- Clarify same-conversation follow-ups after a wait, explain invalid automation targets and payloads, and request owner repair for eligible permanently failed one-time jobs. [#169102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169102) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [erlightbourn-source](https://github.com/erlightbourn-source), and [lonexreb](https://github.com/lonexreb).
- Preserve [restart recovery](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) for eligible dashboard automations interrupted during restart, with explicit stops remaining cancelled. [#170162](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170162) Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).







#### Browser and Computer Use

Browser automation handles more sign-in and upload cases, and Mac automation gains window-specific Peekaboo actions.




##### Browser tabs and sign-in


Ending or resetting one agent's session preserves tabs tracked to another agent, even when their sessions share a name. Older tabs whose owner cannot be identified stay open and may need manual closure, as explained in [browser configuration](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/browser/configuration). The [Chrome extension](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/chrome-extension) also supports uploads totaling less than 47 MiB without local-file access. Uploads of 47 MiB or more still require accessible shared paths and that permission.

When managed Chromium runs without a visible window, it dismisses new modal passkey prompts so the page can accept input and offer another sign-in method. Using passkeys or security keys still requires a browser profile with a visible window. If a request was already waiting before OpenClaw connected, reload the page as described in the [headless passkey guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/browser-login#passkeys-in-headless-browsers).



###### Sources and complete change list


**Tabs and browser controls**

- Keep browser tab cleanup separate for agents with matching session names, including after restart. Older tabs with ambiguous ownership remain open for manual closure. [#167484](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167484), [3f76fe0](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3f76fe021990df309bfcd0905030520bb30f81f4). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prefer untracked or older tabs when enforcing the eight-page limit in locally managed browser profiles. The oldest eligible active tab can still close, and overlapping opens can still lose tabs. [#166666](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166666). Thanks to [@ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [@vokaplok](https://github.com/vokaplok).
- Keep ordinary Chrome tabs available when unusable extension pages or discarded tabs are present. Open a discarded tab to reload it before using it. [#162110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162110). Thanks to [@openclaw-agent-man](https://github.com/openclaw-agent-man), [@dlmccoy](https://github.com/dlmccoy), and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Continue refreshing browser-panel screenshots during sustained typing or scrolling when live streaming is unavailable, and catch up after slow captures. [#153580](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153580). Thanks to [@alexsimeonoww](https://github.com/alexsimeonoww) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Avoid long stalls from wildcard URL filters when selecting responses or waiting for navigation. [#166727](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166727). Thanks to [@SebTardif](https://github.com/SebTardif) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Honor explicitly requested browser-management command timeouts and reject invalid values before connecting. Default management and profile-import timeouts remain unchanged. [#167252](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167252). Thanks to [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

**Setup, uploads and sign-in**

- Restore Chrome-extension uploads below the combined 47 MiB boundary without local-file permission, while retaining the path-based requirements for larger uploads. [#160013](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160013). Thanks to [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@elandesberg](https://github.com/elandesberg), [@holny](https://github.com/holny), and [@Hensarj](https://github.com/Hensarj).
- Dismiss new modal passkey requests in managed headless Chromium so another sign-in method can be selected. [#170857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170857). Thanks to [@trukhinyuri](https://github.com/trukhinyuri) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Recover Chrome automation after a temporary failure cleaning up empty retired extension data, once its background process restarts. Pairing and preferences are preserved; existing recovery blocks still require explicit Disconnect. [#151037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151037). Thanks to [@ethangui](https://github.com/ethangui).
- Find Playwright Chromium on Linux in relative, npm-alias and package-local caches without requiring a manual executable path. [#164659](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164659). Thanks to [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Avoid Chrome-extension relay startup collisions with ports already used by configured browser URLs. [#164678](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164678). Thanks to [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Make browser-tool instructions respect the configured browser node instead of recommending a host override. [#164575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164575). Thanks to [@miguelbranco80](https://github.com/miguelbranco80) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Show declined or expired access requests on the waiting browser page, with a Request again action instead of silently renewing that request. Reloading the page can still request access again. [#164793](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164793). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).






##### Peekaboo window controls


Current Peekaboo CLI and MCP clients using OpenClaw's macOS host can drag in a selected background window, paste rich content and use file dialogs tied to their parent window. The updated host also supports text selection, scrolling at coordinates and menu-bar actions for a chosen app or process.



###### Sources and complete change list


- Support Peekaboo 4.9 window-specific desktop actions through the macOS host. [#166615](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166615). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).







#### Plugins and Integrations

Plugins gain more ways to reload and keep conversations going. This section also covers changes for plugin authors, external coding agents, MCP connections, voice calls, and connected services.




##### Load, update, and reload plugins


An agent can use `reloadAndResume` to end its current turn, reload an authorized plugin, and continue the same conversation with the result on Gateways that support yielding a turn. The continuation is held in memory, so inspect the plugin state before retrying if the Gateway exits. Stopping the conversation cannot undo a completed reload, and compiled bundled changes can still require a restart. See [plugin updates and reloads](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/plugins/uninstall-and-update).

Explicitly enabled standalone plugin files now start their hooks and tool policies. Adding a load path alone does not enable a plugin, and an explicit disable still takes precedence. Normal Gateway restarts preserve persistent plugin session state, while disabling or removing a plugin still deletes that state. Updates also preserve settings when an unsafe plugin path blocks discovery, though the path must be repaired before the plugin can run.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Loading and updates**

- Agents can finish their turn, reload a plugin, and resume the conversation through the explicit `reloadAndResume` action [#171257](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171257). Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Enabled standalone files start their hooks and configured tool policies, using the filename as the plugin ID [#171330](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171330). Thanks [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Source-checkout plugins can load SDK worker helpers again, with Windows nested and lazy aliases resolving from the plugin's captured source [#164662](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164662), [56d714d](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/56d714d0136544b5eff5aab4824fa1e35341c1f9). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Plugin settings and enable, disable, activation, and uninstall commands accept mixed-case plugin IDs [#164683](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164683). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Checkout plugin skills, including Discord and WhatsApp skills, remain available during updates [#165882](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165882). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Stable official ClawHub installs try a build matching the running OpenClaw version, though a fallback can still be incompatible [#167485](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167485). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@charlesBerg-ux](https://github.com/charlesBerg-ux).
- Doctor can finish postponed external-plugin upgrades when the public setup entry provides the required migration detector [#162275](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162275). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Doctor uses updated repair code when a replacement plugin occupies the same install path [#165049](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165049). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@adlbas26](https://github.com/adlbas26), and [@Torsie](https://github.com/Torsie).
- Repair can install replacements during pending migrations and try a trusted same-version npm package during registry lag; installation alone does not activate the plugin [#166588](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166588). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@CodeForcer](https://github.com/CodeForcer).
- Forced replacements can finish activation after active work ends without being cancelled by their own repeated configuration notification [#166618](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166618). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Post-install Doctor can run its repair commands with affected older updaters [#167833](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167833). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@GoDiao](https://github.com/GoDiao).
- Doctor and updates preserve plugin settings, enablement, and policy when an unsafe path blocks discovery [#168111](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168111). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Known incompatible Codex plugins are flagged before use, with clearer update recovery guidance; restart after repair [#168930](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168930). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ezimerman](https://github.com/ezimerman), and [@adlbas26](https://github.com/adlbas26).
- Inspection explains why an incompatible plugin is blocked while keeping healthy plugins inspectable [#168977](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168977). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Deferred official-plugin updates preserve existing webhook callback settings [#169035](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169035). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@sirius1024](https://github.com/sirius1024).
- Deployment guidance identifies npm and git plugin directories that external cleanup scripts must preserve [#165023](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165023). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@morikplay](https://github.com/morikplay).

**Conversations and reloads**

- Reloads that time out waiting for active work retry once the plugin becomes idle; other failures still need a manual reload or configuration reversal [#164894](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164894). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Normal stops and restarts preserve persistent plugin session state [#164975](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164975). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Local and linked plugins can save their own state and use durable incoming-message queues [#165063](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165063). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Selected bundled plugins can reload when a dormant record with the same ID previously blocked them [#167647](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167647). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@mbelinky](https://github.com/mbelinky), [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007), and [@giodl73-repo](https://github.com/giodl73-repo).
- Model-catalog refreshes keep already started chat and scheduled turns running [#167492](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167492). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Chat and cron starts no longer fail when their model catalog changes at the same time [#168410](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168410). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Plugin-added prompt context stays attached to its original turn on later requests and after reopening the conversation [#167938](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167938). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Context-engine summaries and new shared completion requests keep working after unrelated plugin reloads [#168916](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168916). Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- A failed native-provider catalog refresh no longer leaves its rejected configuration in the next request [#169321](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169321). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Browser plugins can import and mount `createSessionHeaderLink` again. It renders no return button and is deprecated for removal after January 12, 2027; use [custom accessories](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/compatibility-policy#session-header-link-helper) [#169746](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169746). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

**Startup and resource use**

- Long-running Gateways and model-catalog workers can release additional retired plugin registry resources [#159448](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159448). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@JFLeonM](https://github.com/JFLeonM), [@Cyb3rb1ade](https://github.com/Cyb3rb1ade), and [@highfly-hi](https://github.com/highfly-hi).

##### Plugin SDK changes and migration


Plugin authors can await session reads, personal model-account operations, tool factories, and transcript preparation. CLI-backed agents keep Gateway tools available across turns with each caller's permissions and context. See the [SDK migration guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/how-to-migrate) for the updated APIs.

Check external-plugin imports before updating. Several published helpers and the legacy JSON dedupe migration APIs have been removed, so plugins importing them can fail to load. Custom runtimes must implement `getSessionEntryAsync` and `getSessionEntryByIdAsync`. Existing synchronous session getters keep their behavior but are deprecated.



###### Sources and complete change list


**Awaited APIs and transcript handling**

- Seven additive Async methods let plugins await personal model-account operations; existing synchronous methods remain available with deprecation warnings [#164630](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164630). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- `runAgentEndSideEffectsAsync` waits for transcript-anchor preparation; use `awaitAgentEndSideEffects` when hook completion must also be awaited [#164747](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164747). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Tool factories can prepare asynchronously, including approval reads, while synchronous factories remain supported [#165012](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165012). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- `readSessionContextAsync` returns complete session context with source-access checks [#165134](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165134). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Transcript write scopes let plugins prepare messages asynchronously; incognito targets require the supported preparation forms [#167715](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167715). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Awaited complete session-entry reads are available, and Active Memory keeps incognito recall debugging transcripts private [#167952](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167952). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Plugin-started turns save their finished status and timing without routine persistence errors or duplicate answers [#167621](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167621). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- CLI-backed agents retain their shared Gateway-tool listener after its first turn, and later turns use their own permissions and transcript context [#165428](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165428), [#165432](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165432). **Hook guidance**

- Hook documentation explains existing `after_tool_call` observation fields and repairs the session-end transcript guidance link; observation hooks cannot enforce tool policy [#149377](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149377), [#161907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161907). Thanks [@arpanghoshal](https://github.com/arpanghoshal), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [@Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx).






##### MCP sign-in and reconnection


Administrators can sign in to installed OAuth MCP plugins from eligible HTTPS dashboards and recover an unfinished terminal sign-in. Local callback handling also accepts a provider registration that omits the port from the otherwise matching loopback URL. During reconnection, disconnected servers are excluded from new tool discovery, while tools already known to the model keep their declarations; calls still fail while the server is unavailable. See [MCP connections](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/mcp) and [dashboard sign-in](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/mcp/control-ui).



###### Sources and complete change list


**Sign-in**

- Eligible HTTPS dashboards can connect installed OAuth MCP plugins without duplicate server configuration and recover unfinished terminal sign-in [#163875](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163875). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- OAuth sign-in can save credentials when a provider such as Dropbox registers the same HTTP loopback callback without its port [#170876](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170876). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@9fw2pq8sgb-art](https://github.com/9fw2pq8sgb-art).

**Reconnection**

- Disconnected servers leave new tool discovery during recovery and return after reconnection, while healthy servers stay available [#165158](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165158). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@BorisZhangYYY](https://github.com/BorisZhangYYY).
- Known tool declarations stay visible to the model during reconnection, with unavailable executions still refused [#167642](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167642). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).






##### External coding agents


External ACP coding agents start under the correct OpenClaw owner and workspace, and aliases reply through their owner's account. Eligible existing conversations can resume when the requester, backend, and target match. Session lookup and resets do less repeated work, and stopping an earlier turn no longer cancels a separate follow-up reply. See [agent sessions](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/acp-agents/sessions), [controls](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/acp-agents/controls), and [reply delivery](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/acp-agents/delivery).

Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@ajmtrz](https://github.com/ajmtrz), and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Configuration commands accept an external agent's primary model selection, with support decided by its harness and native fallback validation retained [#166662](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166662). Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@ftlc-ian](https://github.com/ftlc-ian), and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

Thanks [@saariuslystoned](https://github.com/saariuslystoned), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@josephbergvinson](https://github.com/josephbergvinson), [@lachiefreedom](https://github.com/lachiefreedom), and [@llagoon3](https://github.com/llagoon3).

**Sessions and replies**

Thanks [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng), [@techdou](https://github.com/techdou), and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Resuming an external agent uses indexed matching instead of scanning every retained session [#165550](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165550). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

##### Voice calls, search, Tailscale, and services


- **Voice calls** can take a task brief, let you follow and steer the conversation, and send an outcome report. Reports, live transcripts, callbacks from recent recipients, and Twilio voicemail detection are optional and off by default. Enabled reports include the transcript by default. Spending limits in a brief are model instructions and do not enforce payments. Callback numbers do not prove a caller's identity, and voicemail detection can mistake a person for a machine. Interrupted or uncertain report and transcript sends are not replayed and can be lost. See [call briefs and errands](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/voice-call/realtime-and-streaming#per-call-briefs-and-errands) and the [voice-call CLI](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/voicecall).
- **Search** keeps Firecrawl news and image results, reduces DuckDuckGo challenges caused by browser impersonation, and offers managed web search only after a provider is configured.
- **Tailscale access** can keep an authenticated Gateway available on the same computer when tailnet policy specifically disables Serve; restore the policy and restart for remote access. Previously verified GitHub users can retain profile access during temporary GitHub failures. During an outage, a reassigned GitHub login used by a new tailnet member can still reach the previous owner's profile until a successful check catches the change. See [Tailscale setup](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/tailscale) and [user identities](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/user-model).
- **Connected services** get more consistent meeting transcript exports, preserved Feishu webhook settings during updates, and A2A checks that refuse delivery when an environment-backed credential is missing. Supply the missing variable and reload or restart before retrying A2A.

**Voice calls**

- Calls can use a task brief, live steering, outcome reports, recent-recipient callbacks, and Twilio voicemail handling, alongside current-call hang-up, delayed outbound greetings, and optional idle hang-up [#165715](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165715), [#165716](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165716). Thanks [@mbelinky](https://github.com/mbelinky).
